# CODEMAP

Spec: `docs/CONTRACT.md`. Tools: `tools/save_sync_test.py` (two local launchers, checks the save transfer and the reset), `tools/fake_adapter.py` (fake game adapter), `tools/echo_peer.py` (echoes 0x0100/0x0101 and the pose puppet's 0x0140/0x0141/0x0142 frames back after a delay; see `adapters/re0/README.md`), `tools/spoof_peer.py` (sends a wrong slot byte over local transport), `tools/diagnostics_test.py` (guest log lines and a crash dump reach the host), `tools/build_check_test.py` (mismatched builds, game builds and mod files refuse, matching ones carry on), `tools/peer_link_test.py` (`--gap S`: a fake guest goes silent for S seconds against a host launcher with `fake_adapter.py`, S=5 is held and comes back as a second PEER_UP, S=15 is PEER_DOWN; `--flood N`: N unreliable 0x0100 frames a second show a `Peer budget:` line and the link stays up), `tools/launcher_tests/` (C# console unit tests for the pure launcher rules: mod content hash, BUILD_INFO comparison, member grace, token buckets, fresh-frame queue, adapter option values, lobby codes, refusals and step codec, friend lobbies, retry schedule, peer stats, status words, game watch, command line, log rotation, game profiles, and the file and process rules on temp folders: mod install (single files and folder entries: `ModFilesTests`, `ModInstallerTests`, `ModFolderTests`; `ModProgressTests` for the percent bands, the reporter, the rate bound and the reported steps; `LAUNCHER_TESTS_ONLY=mod` runs them), updater rollback, save sync, session archive, settings, report, instance guard, framing, the adapter bridge on an ephemeral port (tests that touch settings or logs set `COOP_LAUNCHER_DATA`); `dotnet run --project tools/launcher_tests`), `tools/sr2/tunnel_launcher_test.py` (two launchers on local transport and two stand-in adapters that answer the heartbeat: WELCOME id, unicast and broadcast GAME_DATAGRAMs both ways with the source slot, a guest that leaves and returns keeps slot and id, a guest of a native-transport host gets no adapter link; uses `tools/sr2/wire.py`), `tools/sr2/session_test` and `tools/sr2/player_name_test` (C# console tests of `Session` with a scripted lobby and of `PlayerName`; `dotnet run -c Release`). RE0 research tools in `tools/re0/`: `gamectl.py` (keys and screenshots to the game window only, refuses unless RE0 is in front; `idle` = seconds since the user last used mouse/keyboard), `probe.py`, `disasm.py`, `watch_write.py`, `fake_session.py` (stand-in launcher with a scripted peer: doors (with the door target and flags in ROOM_STATE, so a door barrier wait can be seen), rooms, party mode, placements, a join snapshot with `--guest`; prints the game's room script events (EVENT_START, EVENT_STEPS) and APPEARANCE, `appearance <billy|rebecca> <hex>` sends one, `--fake-character` swaps the fake's character; tests split rooms and rejoining on one PC), `party_state.py` (both characters' room record, scene, in-room flag, position), `enemies.py` (live enemies of the loaded room: pool slot, class, HP, position), `dump_stack.py` (registers and return addresses of a `coop\crash-*.dmp`). DS2 research tools in `tools/ds2/` (static ones read `DS2.exe` straight from disk; output goes to the git-ignored `tools/ds2/out/`): `rtti_scan.py` (Decima reflection: types, bases, fields with offsets, message handlers present in the file), `disasm.py` (x64 disassembly, xrefs, string users, MSVC RTTI vtables), `symbols.py` (script-exported function name to address), `probe.py` (read-only live reads: instances of a class (`--near` = only the heap holding an address, seconds instead of minutes), pointer refs, annotated dumps, entity transforms, `handlers <Type>` from the live reflection records), `save_sync_test.py` (DS2 save sync on local transport with temp folders: manifest, every *.dat into the guest's session Documents, reset), `fake_launcher.py` (stand-in launcher with `--crater-set`, `--crater-clear` and `--crater-log` for CRATER_SET; on the adapter port with a scripted peer: stands, walks a circle, reports driving a vehicle for a time window and prints the role of each vehicle report it receives (driver or passenger); `--echo-weapon` plays Sam's WEAPON_STATE / WEAPON_FIRE back as the peer's; `--cutscene` plays a cutscene host (START, GO on READY, END) with `--guest`, or a guest that answers READY, and prints every cutscene message; `--accept-gives` / `--refuse-gives` answer the guest adapter's CARGO_GIVE as a host, `--give-native` sends one as a guest; prints CARGO_ADD, MOVE_ACK and STORY_EVENT with the order row; the real launchers are not needed), `fake_peer.py` (scripted second player: walks a circle around the local player, PLAYER_STATE at 60 Hz; `--cargo` plays a guest's backpack for the give/take menu (`--pickup` asks the host to confirm a pickup), `--host` plays a host (`--host-give` takes the guest's first piece and gives one, `--pickup-answers` accepts or refuses pickups in turn, `--host-pickup` reports a host pickup), `--drop` reports a drop (`here` = next to the local player), drops received are printed, `--drive ID` drives a vehicle along the circle (`--drive-load` reports its bed, `--drive-window` limits the drive to a time window), the vehicle the game drives and its load are printed), `gamectl.py` (DS2 window via `tools/re0/gamectl.py` (the largest DS2.exe window: the start-up launcher can stay visible), plus the shared game-window lock, `launch`, `close` and relative `mouse` motion for the camera). `tools/publish_check.py` gates pushes and releases.

## launcher/ (C# .NET 8, namespace CoopLauncher)

| file | owns | key members |
|---|---|---|
| Program.cs | entry point (a windowed exe; a command-line start attaches to its console): `--webview-check` alone = prints the WebView2 runtime version (or `missing`) and exits 0/1 without a window; any other argument = CLI (holds the `Local\CoopLauncher.Cli.<pid>` mutex for its life, runs the start-up update if the setting is on); no argument = single instance, then the window (the update is staged in the background by the window, never before it); after the window closed and the single-instance handle is released, `Updater.RestartRequested` relaunches the staged build | `Main`, `RunWindow`, `RunCli`, `WebCheckFlag` |
| CliOptions.cs | argument parsing (`--host-options k=v;k=v`: a local join's host option values, which a Steam lobby carries); an unknown or malformed argument is named on stderr as `Unknown argument: X` before the usage; a Steam join's lobby id is read with `LobbyCode` (spaces, a label or a `joinlobby` link are fine) and kept as plain digits, a bad one is refused the same way; unit tested (`CliOptionsTests`) | `CliOptions.Parse`, `Usage` |
| App.cs | wiring and ~100 Hz main loop (partial class: `App.cs`, `App.Status.cs`, `App.Reconnect.cs`); CLI runs one session, interactive (GUI) takes commands and returns to idle; commands `Host`, `Join`, `Leave`, `Invite`, `SwitchToInvite`, `ClearRefusal`, `ResendAvatars`, `StartGame`, `PlaySolo`, `WatchLobbies` queue onto the loop thread; the START GAME gate: a window launcher (`_interactive`) whose game is not running sets `_startHeld` in `StartSession`, `TryLaunch` keeps waiting (`StartGateClosed`, `StartGate.Open`) until the host called `StartGame` or, for a guest, the host member published `StartingGame` / `InGame` (latched in `_hostStarted`); a command-line run is never held and starts at once; `PlaySolo` (host) = `Session.ReleaseHeld` + `Log.Event("Playing solo")`; a build refusal carries the host/you numbers (`BuildInfo.Detail`, `MismatchDetail`); `_sessionStarted` marks a started session (a rendezvous game's session is only a `NativeJoin`: no bridge, session frames, build check, save sync or log forwarding, and it launches with its options at once; `StartBridgedSession` builds the bridge, build check, save sync and log forwarder of a lobby game, or of a rendezvous game whose host chose the bridged transport, and turns a taken loopback port (`PortInUse`) or an unusable save folder (`SaveFolderMissing`) into a refusal while a log forwarder that cannot start only goes unused); `AdapterOptions` = `GameOptions.ForAdapter` for this machine, written by save sync or by `GameLauncher.LaunchWithOptions` (the result maps to a refusal: `LaunchFailed`, `GameUnsupported`, `ModNotInstalled`); `Refuse` records the one refusal (`_refusal`: kind, detail, game, lobby; cleared by the next Host/Join and `ClearRefusal`) and tells the player once with `Log.Event`; `Preflight` = `StartChecks` before a host lobby is opened and before a joined lobby's session starts (skipped with `--no-launch`); the loop throws `SteamLostException` when `SteamBootstrap.Lost`; an invite received in a lobby is kept (`_invite`) and `InviteAccepted` raised; `SteamAvatars` is pumped each tick and wanted for this player, the lobby's members and the friends hosting (`AvatarLoaded`); idle with Steam it reads `FriendLobbies` every 5 s and asks `LobbyDirectory` for each friend lobby's data (`PollDiscovery`), and while the window watches the Lobbies tab (`WatchLobbies`) searches the lobby list now and every `LobbySearchMs` 10 s (results through `LobbyDirectory.Select` into `AppStatus.Lobbies`); a game that was running when a session ended is reset (`AdapterSettings.Reset`) once it exits (`_pendingResets`, checked every 5 s while idle); `DropSession` = everything but the lobby | `Run`, `Host`, `Join`, `Leave`, `Invite`, `SwitchToInvite`, `ClearRefusal`, `ResendAvatars`, `Stop`, `MarkOffline`, `StatusChanged`, `AvatarLoaded`, `InviteAccepted`, `StartSession`, `TryLaunch`, `StartGateClosed`, `StartGame`, `PlaySolo`, `WatchLobbies`, `PollDiscovery`, `Refuse`, `EndSession`, `DropSession` |
| App.Status.cs | the status the loop publishes and the step it tells the lobby: `LocalProgress` (`SessionProgress.Step` from the loop's state, `StartHeld` while the launch waits for the start gate), `PublishStep` (writes `cf_step` only when the step, percent or refusal changed), `PublishOptions` (writes `cf_options`, this player's own option values (`GameOptions.OwnValues`: the outfits), when the lobby or the settings were replaced and the text changed), `CurrentStatus` (players with their game line, published step, ping, held and silent; `PeerConnected` only while every other slot is in game and none held or silent; `Rejoining` / `WaitingForHost` with the lobby being rebuilt and the seconds left, never a copy of the old status), `PublishLobbyData` (host only, when the text changed: the roster `RosterCodec.Encode` of the players and the start of play `cf_started`, Unix seconds when the host's own step first is `InGame`), `TrackTimes` (`LobbyOpenedAtMs`, `StepSinceMs`, the lobby's own `Listing` kept through a rebuild), `HoldSecondsLeft` per player (`Session.HoldMsLeft`), the save meter's file and bytes into `SaveFile` / `SaveBytes` / `SaveTotal`, `ViewOf` (seat count and characters worked out once per lobby and host options) | `PublishStatus`, `CurrentStatus`, `PublishStep`, `PublishLobbyData`, `TrackTimes`, `ViewOf` |
| App.Reconnect.cs | the paths on which a session outlives its lobby (the session, the game link and the running game are kept, `Session.Hold`): a guest's lobby failed and is rejoined (`Rejoin`, 5 s / 2 min, also follows the host's new lobby), a guest's host left (`WatchHost`, `WaitForHost`: the old lobby is left, `HostFollow.Find` every 2 s looks for the host's new lobby for 180 s, then `HostLeft`), the host's own lobby failed (`HoldSession`, `ReopenLobby`: a new lobby with the same game and host options every 5 s for 180 s, then `SteamOffline`); `RejoinHeldSession` rebinds the held session to any lobby whose owner is the session's host, a lobby with another owner drops the session; Host/Join are refused while any of the three runs | `HoldSession`, `WatchHost`, `WaitForHost`, `ReopenLobby`, `RejoinHeldSession`, `TryRejoin`, `FollowHost` |
| StartChecks.cs | what must hold before a lobby opens or a joined session starts (`Refusal?` result): game found, build supported, required file present, mod installed (an installed but outdated mod is installed again first while the game is not running) | `StartChecks.Run` |
| AppStatus.cs | display status of the loop: `AppState` (idle, connecting, hosting, joined, game running, peer connected + RTT, rejoining, waiting for host, offline + `OfflineReason`), the open lobby's game, `Players` (`PlayerSlot`: id, name, host / local, the game's own line, published `SessionStep` and percent, ping, held, silent, the encoded option values it published (`Options`), the whole seconds left of a hold (`HoldSecondsLeft`)), `Refusal` (why the last start was refused, until the next Host/Join), `Step` and `SavePercent` of this launcher, `Seats` and `HostOptions` of the lobby, `RejoinAttempt` / `SecondsLeft`, `LaunchedAtMs`, `FriendLobbies` (`FriendLobby`, each with its `LobbyListing` once known), `Invite` (`PendingInvite`), `SaveFile` / `SaveBytes` / `SaveTotal` (the save arriving), `LobbyOpenedAtMs`, `StepSinceMs`, `SessionStartedUnix` (host-published start of play), `Listing` (the open lobby's own data), `Lobbies` / `LobbiesSearching` (the Lobbies tab's list); `Bridged` = the session has the adapter link; `Equals` compares every member | `AppStatus`, `AppState`, `PlayerSlot`, `FriendLobby`, `PendingInvite`, `OfflineReason` |
| Updater.cs | self-update from the rolling release `latest` (`version.txt` build, read through ReleaseFeed): download the whole package (launcher, game profiles, every game's adapter), write the `update.pending` marker, move old files to `*.old`, copy new, check the new `version.txt`, delete the marker; any failure moves every `*.old` back and deletes the new copies ("Update skipped"); at the next `Stage` an existing marker means the replace was cut short and is rolled back, otherwise leftover `*.old` are deleted; start-up CLI relaunches (`TryInstall`), the window stages (`Stage`, sets `Staged`) and Program relaunches after the window closed when `RestartRequested` (`RelaunchStaged`); `Stage` takes an optional `IProgress<ModProgress>` (the download by bytes, then installing); a lock per package folder (`TryAcquireLock`) keeps a start-up `Recover` in one process from rolling back a replace under way in another (the second skips its update); skipped silently on any failure or outside the packaged layout | `TryInstall`, `Stage`, `Staged`, `RestartRequested`, `RelaunchStaged`, `Replace`, `Recover` |
| ReleaseFeed.cs | the one cached read of the GitHub release (`update.json` repo): read at startup, forced only by the Update button; a failed read is logged and keeps the old result; also the shared HTTP client | `Read`, `Get`, `AssetName` |
| ModInstaller.cs | a game's mod = its profile adapterFiles (folder entries already expanded to files) in the game folder. Ownership: marker `.cfown` records the file's hash and equals it, or the file equals the package copy (an install cut short). `Status` (not installed / installed / update available / modified = our marker but another hash: another mod or damage), `Install` (also update and repair: stages every file as `.tmp`, backs up a foreign file as `.cfbak`, `.cfbak2`, ... never overwriting, moves all into place with markers, removes the files a folder entry's manifest lists that the package dropped (`Removal`; a dropped file the player changed is left), rolls every file and the manifest back on a failure; build in `.cfbuild`), `Uninstall` (restores `.cfbak`, leaves a file another mod put in place, deletes the AdapterIni folder inside the game folder except `archive` and a folder that still holds the player's files, after archiving the session saves through `AdapterSettings.Reset`; empty mod folders and `.cfmod` are pruned; a session folder that could not be archived is kept), `Install` and `Uninstall` take an optional `IProgress<ModProgress>`: install runs check, back up, copy, verify (each copy re-hashed against the package, a mismatch rolls everything back), remove dropped files; uninstall runs remove; `Placement` / `Removal` are the per-file steps; `ContentHash` (SHA-256 over the profile's expanded adapter files as installed: per file lowercase dst with `/`, 0x00, u64 LE length, bytes; null when one is missing). Bookkeeping of a folder entry's files (marker, backup, staging, manifest `.cfmanifest`) is under `<parent>/.cfmod/<folder>` so the game's folder holds only mod files (SR2's adapter hashes every file of `coop\scripts`) | `Status`, `Install`, `Uninstall`, `ContentHash`, `ModState` |
| InstanceGuard.cs | which other launcher processes may be closed: never a command-line run (it holds `Local\CoopLauncher.Cli.<pid>`), never the window that answers, others only when hung (not responding or without a window); the rule is the pure `ShouldClose`; run by SingleInstance | `ShouldClose`, `CloseOtherLaunchers`, `IsCliRun`, `CliMutexName` |
| SingleInstance.cs | one launcher window per user session: named mutex; the focus listener is registered inside `Acquire` (answers a second start at once, queues the bring-forward until `OnShowRequested`); a second start asks the running window to come forward and exits, waits `HungAfterMs` more for an answer, and only closes a window that is provably hung (InstanceGuard) before taking over, else exits without killing anything; an update relaunch waits for its predecessor (`COOP_LAUNCHER_PREDECESSOR`) | `Acquire`, `OnShowRequested`, `HungAfterMs` |
| AppData.cs | `%AppData%\CoopLauncher` paths (or the folder named by the environment variable `COOP_LAUNCHER_DATA`, which tests and the UI harness point at a temp folder): `settings.json`, `logs\launcher.log` (previous run `launcher.prev.log`), `art\<game>\` (outfit pictures taken from a game's files, `OutfitArt`), `webview2\` (the WebView2 user data); `StampFormat` = the one timestamp format for archives, reports and peer logs | `SettingsFile`, `LogsDir`, `ArtDir`, `WebViewDir`, `LogFile`, `PreviousLogFile`, `StampFormat`, `DataRootVariable` |
| AppSettings.cs | the launcher's settings (check for updates at start, per-game game folder, per-game option choices, window bounds, last game, `LobbyVisibility`: who can find the lobbies this launcher hosts, friends only by default): read once, written on every change (to `settings.json.tmp`, then moved over the file; the replaced readable file stays as `settings.json.bak` and is the fallback when the file is unreadable); every `Update` calls `GameFolders.Invalidate` | `AppSettings.Current`, `Read`, `Update`, `WithGameFolder`, `Option`, `WithOption`, `LastGame`, `WithLastGame`, `LobbyVisibility`, `WithLobbyVisibility` |
| GameFolders.cs | a game's folder: the settings override, else Steam's (`SteamLibrary.FindGameDir`); a found folder is remembered per game id until `Invalidate` (a game not found is looked for again) | `Find`, `Invalidate` |
| ParentConsole.cs | command-line starts attach to the starting console (the exe is windowed, no console flash) | `Attach` |
| Gui/GuiHost.cs | GUI entry: log file, unhandled UI errors logged, App loop on a background thread (a loop failure is logged once per distinct failure: the message on screen, the stack trace in the file only (`Log.FileOnly`); `Program.cs` logs the CLI one in full) (retried every 3 s while it cannot start: state Offline, with `OfflineReason.SteamNotRunning` when Steam is not running or went away), window on the STA thread: the web window (`Web/WebShell`) after `WebRuntime.EnsureOrExplain`, or with `-p:LauncherUi=Classic` (defines `CLASSIC_UI`) the WinForms `MainForm`; this is the one `#if` | `Run`, `UiAvailable`, `CreateWindow` |
| Gui/MainForm.cs, Gui/MainForm.Screens.cs | the window in the broadcast tool's operator look (a ChromelessForm; the top bar is its caption): composes the top bar, one `ScreenHost` (home, lobbies, joining, session, settings, select), the log drawer (Home and Lobbies only), the `ModalLayer` and the mini window; keeps the `Intent` (HOST or a join), the `JoinTarget` (typed code, friend card, lobby row, popup), the tab and the open view; `Apply(status)` works out the screen (`Screens.For`), fills it, starts or stops the lobby search (`App.WatchLobbies`) and the one-second timer (only while a session or a lobby popup needs it); mod states re-read in the background on activation (2 s throttle), after install / uninstall / folder change and when a session ends; Cancel = Leave + ClearRefusal + intent reset; the fix buttons of the notice row and the joining screen (Install / Update / Repair, Update to N, Browse, Join again, Switch, Report); game watch (Relaunch, `Log.Event` when the game exits); mini mode (MINI MODE opens `MiniWindow` and hides this window, Expand, the session ending or an invite closes it and brings this one back, Play solo); Esc closes the popup, then the view | `MainForm.Apply`, `ScreenFor`, `ShowView`, `OpenModal`, `ToggleOptions`, `OpenLobbyPopup`, `RunLobbyAction`, `StartMini`, `ExpandMini`, `RunNoticeFix` |
| Gui/Theme.cs | palette (`Raised`, `Disabled`, the state tints `TintGreen/Amber/Red`, `ScrimAlpha`, `CardOverArtAlpha`), fonts (Title, Name, NameTall, NameSmall, Step, StepStrong, StepSmall, Hero, CodeLarge, CodeMedium, MonoMedium, MonoBody, MonoStep, Initial, InitialLarge, InitialSmall; point sizes are px x 0.75) and metrics (colour on state only: green connected, red problem, amber in progress; shared primary/tool button widths, content max width, arrow size, top resize band); every pixel metric goes through `Scale` (system DPI), fractional ones (animated offsets, blur radii) through `ScaleF` | `Theme`, `Scale`, `ScaleF`, `Unscale` |
| Gui/Draw.cs | shared painting: text (padded and `TightWidth`, letter-spaced, `Ascent`), paths (middle ellipsis), captions, lamps, arrows, separators, fills, cover/fit/faded images (centred or anchored to the top), the screen backdrop, wrapped text (left or right aligned), the thin scroll position mark, and the glyphs: success `Check` (drawn as far as a share of its length), `Cross`, `HollowCircle`, `Dashed` border, `Lock`, `Expand`; `ArrowDirection` Up, Down, Left, Right | `Draw.Cover`, `Draw.Faded`, `Draw.Backdrop`, `Draw.Arrow`, `Draw.Check`, `Draw.Cross`, `Draw.Dashed`, `Draw.Path`, `Draw.Wrapped`, `Draw.TextSpaced`, `Draw.ScrollThumb` |
| Gui/ChromelessForm.cs | base window without the system title bar: WM_NCCALCSIZE gives the title bar to the client (maximized: client = work area), WM_NCHITTEST answers the top resize band and the caption strip; native side/bottom borders, shadow, Snap, Alt+F4, animations kept; size it with `SizeFromClientSize` | `ChromelessForm`, `CaptionHeight` |
| Gui/NcHitTest.cs | WM_NCHITTEST message, answers and lParam screen point | `NcHitTest.ScreenPoint` |
| Gui/WindowButtons.cs | minimize and close at the right of the top bar: line glyphs, grey hover fading in and out (close darker), the top resize band passes through | `WindowButtons` |
| Gui/NativeTheme.cs | Windows' dark frame (dark mode, border in the line colour) and dark native scrollbars | `DarkFrame`, `DarkScrollbars` |
| Gui/SteamArt.cs | a game's capsule, hero and logo: the player's custom Steam art (`SteamLibrary.CustomArtFiles`, userdata config grid) first, then Steam's local librarycache; `FindFile` is the lookup (the web window serves the file itself) | `SteamArt`, `SteamArt.Kind`, `FindFile` |
| Gui/TopBar.cs | top strip and title bar (its empty surface hit-tests transparent so the window drags by it), no state words or lamp: brand, the Play / Lobbies `SegmentTabs` and the build (`Build N` / `Dev build`) on Home and Lobbies (`TopBarMode.Browse`), the brand alone in a session, Back before the brand while a view is open (Settings hides), the update button while a newer build is offered, Settings, window buttons | `TopBar.Show(mode, build)`, `Tabs`, `BackButton`, `SettingsButton`, `ShowUpdate` |
| Gui/GameRail.cs | left rail of games with capsule art and each game's mod status line; the selection fill slides between rows, hover fades, locked rows show their art at 35 %, a wheel scrolls by a row with a thin position mark, Up/Down move the selection, list/list item accessibility; selection, Running mark, locks during a session; the row's sub line, status tone and lock come from `RailModel` | `GameRail`, `SetStatus`, `SelectedStatus`, `StatusOf` |
| Gui/GameStatus.cs | what a game's mod state says: the rail line (`Game not found`, `Game build N unsupported`, `<name> not found` for a missing profile `requires` file, `Not installed`, `Mod N`, `Mod update M`, `Mod modified`), the settings line as two items (`SettingsMod`: `Mod 412 installed`, `Mod installed`, `Mod not installed`, `Mod update 413`, `Mod modified`; `SettingsGame`: `Game build 17178773`, `Game found`, `Game not found`), the one mod button (`ModAction`: Install, Update or Repair, none when the mod is installed; disabled while the game runs), `CanHost` (game, mod, supported build, required files), `Read` (hashes files, so off the UI thread) | `GameStatus.Line`, `SettingsMod`, `SettingsGame`, `SettingsLine`, `Action`, `CanHost`, `Read` |
| Gui/HeroBanner.cs | the home hero: the selected game's art 296 px tall (left shade, bottom fade, logo) with the action row on it (HOST or LAUNCH, mod button, `Lobby code` field + Join, options cog, Characters glyph for a game with a character select) and the notice row under it (the hero grows by it) | `HeroBanner.Show`, `RefuseCode`, `OptionsOpen`, `HostClicked`, `JoinRequested`, `OptionsClicked`, `CharactersClicked` |
| Gui/SessionCards.cs | the pure mapping from `AppStatus` to what the home screen says: `HomeView` (which buttons are usable, the mod button, the friends, the notice), the friends hosting (`FriendCard` with the game's name, `n/max` from the listing and whether the game is the selected one), the notice (refusal words with Report, the invite, or `Steam not running` with Start Steam while the app is offline for that reason) and its fix (`NoticeFix`: the fix table), resolved refusals | `SessionCards.Home`, `Friends`, `FixFor`, `NoticeFor`, `Resolved`, `NoticeFix` |
| Gui/HomeScreen.cs | the Play tab: the `GameRail` on the left, the pane beside it with the hero and the FRIENDS PLAYING caption over the `FriendCards`; the pane scrolls when they outgrow it | `HomeScreen.Show`, `Rail`, `OptionsOpen`, `RefuseCode` |
| Gui/UpdateFlow.cs | the window's one update path: reads the release in the background, offers a newer build (`Update to N`), installs it in the background (by itself while no session is open when `CheckForUpdates` is on, else on the click) through `Updater.Stage` (the button reads `Downloading N%` from its progress), then waits for the player's restart (`Restart`); a failed install returns to the offer | `UpdateFlow.Check`, `Click`, `SetFree`, `Label`, `Enabled`, `NewerBuild`, `RestartRequested` |
| Gui/WindowFlash.cs | flashes the window's taskbar button until it comes to the front (an invite arrived while it was behind) | `WindowFlash.Start` |
| Gui/FlatButton.cs | flat squared button: Primary, Normal, Ghost and Icon (both transparent over what is behind them: an outline, or a bare Segoe MDL2 glyph; a fill on hover, Icon also when `Active`), `Kind` can change; fill and ink fade on hover, a press snaps darker; a disabled Primary is the selection gray with muted ink; keyboard focus (Tab, Space/Enter click, 1 px focus border), a dialog button (`IButtonControl`); optional arrow before the label (Back) and after it (`ArrowAfter`); a button without a label centres its two arrows (the select view's swap button) | `FlatButton` |
| Gui/FieldBox.cs | card-grey one-line text field (height and font set by the caller) with focus border; `Refuse` turns the border amber and shakes the field, holds 3 s, fades back (typing reverts at once) | `FieldBox.Input`, `Refuse` |
| Gui/FlatSelect.cs | flat dropdown in the tool's greys, painted (no ComboBox): closed look with the large arrow, `Items` / `SelectedIndex` / `SelectedIndexChanged`, keyboard (Up/Down change, Space/Enter/F4/Alt+Down open), no wheel change; its list is a `SelectMenu` | `FlatSelect` |
| Gui/FlatToggle.cs | the broadcast tool's switch (46x24 track, square thumb that slides, selection-grey track when on) with a label; Space toggles, focus border, check-button accessibility | `FlatToggle.Checked`, `Flipped` |
| Gui/SectionPanel.cs | captioned pane section with a separator, content capped at the max content width, sized to its content on request (no content = no height) | `SectionPanel`, `FitToContent`, `FittedHeight` |
