| Session.cs | slots, epochs, membership diffs, frame routing (a frame for a slot with no peer is dropped); WELCOME carries the local transport id; test: `tools/sr2/session_test` (scripted lobby and transport; compiles `ILobby.cs` with `LobbyListing.cs`, `GameOptions.cs`, `AppSettings.cs` and what they need, so a new `ILobby` member is added to its `ScriptedLobby`; stand-in adapter that answers the heartbeat; `dotnet run -c Release`); a member missing from the lobby is held with its slot and frames before PEER_DOWN (`MemberGrace.PeerGraceMs` 10 s, the host `HostGraceMs` 180 s) and PEER_UP again for the same slot when it returns in time; the lobby owner changing does not end the session: `HostMissing` (a guest's host is not a member or not the owner) is for the app to act on; `RttOf`, `IsSilent`, `IsHeld`, `HoldMsLeft` per member; `ReleaseHeld` (the host gives up every held partner: `MemberGrace.ExpireAll`, PEER_DOWN on the next pump); `Log.Event` lines `<name> joined / left / reconnecting / back`; `Hold` (a lobby failed: every peer held) and `Rebind` (any lobby, same slots, PEER_UP again for every peer); `HostId`, `IsHost`, `LobbyId`, `Held`, `HasPeer`; peer game frames pass `PeerBudget` before the bridge; the budget drop report every 5 s; a profile with `freshTypes` holds its unreliable frames of those types in a `FreshQueue` until the end of the next `Pump` and sends them on `TransportChannel.Fresh` (newest per peer and type wins; everything else goes out at once as before), logs `Fresh channel: N types` at start and `Fresh channel: N older frames replaced` when the count grew | `ApplyMembership`, `Hold`, `Rebind`, `HostMissing`, `RttOf`, `IsSilent`, `IsHeld`, `HoldMsLeft`, `ReleaseHeld`, `OnPeerFrame`, `OnAdapterFrame`, `RouteToPeer`, `FlushFresh`, `End` |
| FreshQueue.cs | pure newest-wins queue for a profile's `freshTypes` (unit tested): holds an unreliable frame of a fresh type per (peer, type), a newer one replaces it (`Replaced` counts them), reliable frames and other types are never held; `ParseTypes` reads hex names and refuses types below 0x0100; `ForProfile` = null without `freshTypes` | `FreshQueue.Accepts`, `Enqueue`, `Drain` (an empty queue returns at once: every pump calls it), `ParseTypes`, `ForProfile` |
| MemberGrace.cs | pure grace state (unit tested): `Update(slotMembers, present, now, graceFor)` says who is held, who returned inside the grace (resync, not reset) and who expired, each member with its own grace (`PeerGraceMs` 10 s, `HostGraceMs` 180 s); `ExpiryDue(now, graceFor)`, `HeldIds`, `Clear`; `ExpireAll()` ends the grace of everyone held (the next `Update` expires them), `MsLeft(id, now, graceFor)` is the time before a hold ends (0 when not held) | `MemberGrace.Update`, `ExpiryDue`, `HeldIds`, `ExpireAll`, `MsLeft`, `PeerGraceMs`, `HostGraceMs` |
| PeerBudget.cs | token buckets on peer game frames (unit tested): `TokenBucket` (integer math, elapsed capped at 60 s), `PeerBudget` per source slot unreliable 300/s burst 600 + 256 KiB/s burst 512 KiB, reliable 300/s burst 3000 + 512 KiB/s burst 4 MiB; a frame must fit both of its buckets or it is dropped and counted per (slot, type, reliability); launcher-owned frames are not budgeted | `PeerBudget.Admit`, `Remove`, `TakeReport`, `TokenBucket` |
| SlotAssigner.cs | owner = 0, rest sorted by id | `Assign` |
| LoopbackBridge.cs | adapter TCP link: HELLO check, heartbeat, timeout, relay; `SaveChanged` event for SAVE_CHANGED 0x0060; every connection has an outgoing queue drained by a writer thread, so `Send` never blocks the loop (unreliable frames are dropped above `UnreliableDropBytes` 256 KiB and counted, the link drops above `MaxQueuedBytes` 4 MiB with "send queue full", a write failure reaches the loop as a disconnect); a new local connection stays pending and replaces the link only after a valid HELLO (a pending one without HELLO is closed after the link timeout, a rejected one gets REJECT) | `Pump`, `Send`, `AdapterReady`, `GameFrame`, `Port`, `DroppedUnreliable` |
| Framing.cs | frame record, wire/loopback codec, message type constants; `Reliable(type, payload)` + `NoSlot` = the one builder of launcher-owned reliable frames | `Frame`, `Msg`, `Framing`, `Reliable` |
| ControlMessages.cs | control payload builders/parsers (all through `Framing.Reliable`, timestamps unreliable); `Welcome` = slot, host slot, max players, epoch, u64 local id (15 bytes) | `Welcome`, `PeerUp`, `TryParseHello` |
| PeerStats.cs | ping schedule, EWMA RTT per slot (`RttOf`, the slowest as `RttMs`), last answer per slot (`SilentFor`: no pong for 5 s, a slot never answered counts from its first ping), stats report; the clock is injectable (unit tested) | `Tick`, `OnPong`, `RttOf`, `SilentFor`, `Remove` |
| ITransport.cs | peer carrier interface; `Send(peer, frame, channel)` with `TransportChannel` Main (0) / Fresh (1) | `ITransport`, `TransportChannel` |
| SteamTransport.cs | ISteamNetworkingMessages channels 0 (Main) and 1 (Fresh), both received, member-only sessions | `Send`, `Pump` |
| LocalTransport.cs | UDP loopback transport + presence keepalive; one lane, the channel is ignored | `PeerPresent` |
| ILobby.cs | membership source interface; `Failure` = `LobbyFailure` (the refusal kind and the log line); `HostOptions` = the host's option values every member reads; `JoinSecret` = a rendezvous game's session password; `SetMemberData` / `MemberData` with `MemberKey` = what each member publishes (`GameStatus`: the game's own status line; `Step`: the session step, `StepCodec`; `Options`: the member's own option values, `GameOptions.OwnValues` encoded); `SetLobbyData` (owner only) with `LobbyKey` = what the host keeps up to date in the lobby's own data (`Roster`, `Started`); `Listing` = the lobby as its own data describes it (`LobbyListing`) | `ILobby`, `MemberKey`, `LobbyKey`, `LobbyMember` |
| SteamLobby.cs | Steam lobby create/join (every string Steam hands back goes through `Text`: Steamworks.NET returns null for missing lobby/member data and persona names, the rest of the launcher sees empty), cf_* data (`LobbyKeys`, all written by the host on create: `cf_app` = the marker `coop-launcher` the lobby list filters on, `cf_host` (persona name), `cf_mod` (installed mod build), `cf_gbuild` (installed game build), `cf_loc` (`SteamNetworkingUtils` ping location, published as soon as Steam knows it), and `cf_roster` / `cf_started` kept up to date through `SetLobbyData`; the lobby type is friends only or public by `AppSettings.LobbyVisibility`; `Listing` is decoded again when the data or the members change; `cf_ver` = the launcher build, refused on entry with `LauncherBuild` (detail `<host>/<you>`, `RefusalVersions`) when both builds are numbered and differ; `cf_join_pw`: the host's nine-digit session password for a rendezvous game; member data `cf_status` (game line), `cf_step` (session step) and `cf_options` (the member's own option values, so a partner's outfit shows in the character select)); the lobby takes `GameOptions.Seats` members; every `Failure` carries a `RefusalKind` (Steam's answer through `Refusals`, a request Steam rejects or never answers (30 s) = `SteamOffline`, not a co-op lobby, a game this launcher has no profile for = `UnknownGame`; "no longer in the lobby" is a connection loss the app rejoins); `HostOptions` is decoded again only when the lobby text changes | `Create`, `Join`, `Pump`, `SetLobbyData`, `Listing` |
| LocalLobby.cs | fake lobby for local testing (no join secret, the local member's data and the host's `SetLobbyData` kept in memory, `Listing` decoded from them with two seats) | `LocalLobby` |
| SteamBootstrap.cs | SteamAPI init (`SteamUnavailableException` carries Steam's init result, `NoSteamClient` = Steam is not running), callbacks, overlay invites (`PendingInvite` = lobby and the inviting friend), `Lost` when Steam announces its shutdown or `IsSteamRunning` is false (checked every 3 s; the loop then throws `SteamLostException` and the window shows Steam not running), `PersonaName` | `PendingInvite`, `Lost`, `Pump`, `PersonaName`, `SteamNetworkingUtils.InitRelayNetworkAccess` once after init (the ping estimates need it), `SteamUnavailableException`, `SteamLostException` |
| ModProgress.cs | what a mod install, uninstall or launcher update reports: `ModStep` (Checking, BackingUp, Copying, Verifying, RemovingOld, Removing, Downloading, Installing, Done, Failed), `ModProgress` (step, percent of the run, file i of n, reason), `ProgressMath` (each step owns a band of the percent, byte-weighted inside it; only Done is 100), `ProgressReporter` (Begin / Advance / AddBytes / Finish / Fail; monotonic; a step with no files is skipped), `ThrottledProgress` (forwards a change of step, Done and Failed at once and other reports at most every 50 ms) | `ProgressMath.Percent`, `ProgressReporter`, `ThrottledProgress` |
| ModFiles.cs | pure rules of a mod's file list: `Expand` (an `adapterFiles` entry with `"folder": true` becomes one `AdapterFile` per file, recursive, ordinal order, `FolderDst` set; a missing or empty folder stays one entry so the package reads as incomplete), `SidePath` / `BookkeepingDir` / `ManifestPath`, the manifest text (`FormatManifest`, `ParseManifest`, `Removed` = listed files the package dropped, only inside the folder), `Decide` (whole-mod `ModState` from `FileFacts`: any file missing that an install put there = not installed, modified outranks update, a new package file or a dropped one still installed = update); `ModFile` is the on-disk file with its bookkeeping paths | `Expand`, `Decide`, `Removed`, `FormatManifest`, `ParseManifest` |
| GameProfile.cs | `games/<id>.json` loader, `adapterFiles` (`src`, `dst`, optional `folder`: a folder expanded by `ModFiles.Expand` at load), `adapterIni` (the adapter ini in the game folder), optional `sessionKind` (`lobby` default; `native` = the game matches players itself, so no lobby, transport, seats, save sync or adapter link: the launcher installs the mod, writes the options and starts the game; `rendezvous` = native plus a Steam lobby that says who hosts, with `joinFile` and `statusFile` beside the adapter ini: `NativeJoin.cs`, no bridge, frames or save sync), optional `requires` (files the game folder must hold, e.g. Juiced's `DFEngine.dll`, with the name the status line uses), optional `freshTypes` (hex frame types that go on the Fresh channel, newest wins; re0: 0x0140, 0x0160, 0x0173; DS2 and SR2 have none), optional `supportedBuilds` (Steam build ids the adapter fits), optional `saveSync` block (fixed Steam-cloud files, or `hostSaveDir`/`guestSaveDir`/`filePattern`), optional `options` (`GameOption`: key, label, `host` flag, `settings` flag (shown in the settings view, not the options grid), values; a value with `dlcAppId` is offered only when `<game>\DLC\<id>` exists; an outfit option names its `character` (a value of the character option) and a value may carry its own `art`; `HasCharacterSelect` = some option has a `character`); optional `outfitArt` {arc, texture}: where a value's picture is in the game folder (`{value}` is replaced by the option value; a `dlcAppId` value looks in `DLC\<id>\costume_*`), `ArtSource` {arc, texture, crop, feather} for a picture that is cropped and feathered (RE0 Wesker); `bridgeWhen` {key, value}: a rendezvous game also gets the bridge, build check and log forwarding when the host's option `key` equals `value` (SR2: transport=steam); `Bridged(hostValues)` is true for a lobby game; `Load` returns one cached instance per profile (the window and the loop share it) and refuses a bad `freshTypes` entry at load; `ListIds` is read once; unit tested (`GameProfileTests`, `ModFilesTests`) | `Load`, `ListIds`, `NativeSession`, `RendezvousSession`, `MissingRequirement`, `HasCharacterSelect`, `GameOption.AvailableIn` |
| GameOptions.cs | the player's option values (settings choice if still offered, else the first value): all for the adapter ini, host options for the lobby (`cf_opts`; a duplicate key keeps its last value), seat characters with the host's character first, `Seats` (the host's `max_players` when the profile offers it and it is 1 to the profile's maximum, else the maximum), `OwnValues` (the options that are not the host's, which `cf_options` carries), `InCharacterSelect` (the character option and each outfit option of a game with a character select: the select view has them, the options grid does not), `OutfitOf` (a character's outfit option), `CharacterValue` (the character option's value for a label) and `ForAdapter` (the values the adapter ini gets: a guest takes the host's value of every host option the lobby carries, so `partner_mode`, `character` and `max_players` match on both machines) | `Current`, `HostValues`, `OwnValues`, `ForAdapter`, `Encode`, `Decode`, `SeatCharacters`, `Seats`, `SeatsKey`, `CharacterKey`, `InCharacterSelect`, `OutfitOf`, `CharacterValue` |
| SaveSyncCoordinator.cs | save sync per session: host sender or guest receiver, launch gate; the guest takes the host id from `session.HostId`; `TimedOut` = `SaveSyncTimeout.TimedOut`: no host frame within 60 s of creation, or none for 30 s once frames arrived and the set is incomplete; a host without a resolvable save folder (or a guest without its guest folder) makes `Create` throw `InvalidOperationException`; the receiver gets `GameLauncher.IsRunning` at creation; host re-sends the save to every peer on SAVE_CHANGED, and (profiles with `filePattern`) sends every save its game writes later, found by SaveWatcher; guest moves staged saves into the session folder while the game exe is not running | `Create`, `Ready`, `TimedOut`, `EnableAdapter`, `Pump`, `SaveSyncTimeout` |
| LogForwarder.cs | guest diagnostics to the host: new adapter-log bytes every 2 s (LOG_APPEND 0x0050) and any crash-*.dmp written during the session (CRASH_DUMP 0x0051, 32 KiB chunks, retried while still being written); host writes one `peer_<steamid>_<stamp>.log` per peer and session (newest 10 per peer kept) and `peer_<steamid>_<dump>` beside its adapter log, creating the folder; a disk error on either side skips that pass | `Create`, `Pump`, `ExpiredPeerLogs`, `IsPeerLog` |
| BuildCheck.cs | same build on every machine: host sends BUILD_INFO 0x0013 as a peer joins; a guest on a different build stops before the game starts (dev builds only warn); shared `LocalBuild` reads version.txt every time (the updater re-reads it after installing; also written to the lobby as `cf_ver`); BUILD_INFO also carries the installed game build id (Steam buildid); a guest on another game build stops before the game starts; BUILD_INFO also carries the SHA-256 of the installed mod files (`ModInstaller.ContentHash`, zero = not installed): `BuildInfo.Compare` returns the first difference as `BuildMismatch` (launcher build, game build, mod files, mod not installed; a dev build is guarded by the hash alone), `Reason` beside the log text `Mismatch` (the app turns it into a refusal with `Refusals.FromBuildMismatch`); `Compared` = the two `BuildInfo` of that comparison and `BuildInfo.Detail` the refusal's `<host>/<you>` numbers (launcher builds, game builds, or the host's `cf_mod` against the installed mod build) | `BuildCheck.Create`, `LocalBuild`, `Ready`, `Mismatch`, `Reason`, `Compared`, `BuildInfo.Compare`, `BuildInfo.Detail` |
| Rejoin.cs | `RetrySchedule` (pure, unit tested: attempts due once per interval from one interval after the start, `GaveUp`, `SecondsLeft`) and `Rejoin`: a guest whose lobby fails (Steam: no longer listed in the lobby = dropped) retries joining the same lobby every 5 s for 2 min with the session held (`Session.Hold`); the running game is left alone (GameLauncher skips launch and install while it runs) and the join snapshot catches it up; the host's re-hosting and the wait for the host use a `RetrySchedule` too | `RetrySchedule`, `Rejoin`, `App.TryRejoin` |
| HostFollow.cs | a guest polls its host's published lobby (2 s): `Find(excludeLobbyId)` = the lobby the host publishes for the same game, `MovedTo` = the same once the host is no longer a member (a new lobby for the same game: host crashed or relaunched) | `HostFollow.Find`, `MovedTo`, `App.FollowHost`, `App.WaitForHost` |
| LobbyPresence.cs | the host's open lobby and game as Steam rich presence (`cf_lobby`, `cf_game`), set by SteamLobby.Create, cleared on dispose | `Publish`, `Clear`, `Read` |
| Refusal.cs | why a start was refused: `RefusalKind`, `Refusal` (kind, detail, game, lobby), `LobbyFailure` (a lobby that cannot be used), the `BuildMismatch` enum, `RefusalVersions` (`Format(host, you)` / `TryParse` of the detail `<host>/<you>` that LauncherBuild, GameBuild and ModFiles carry), and the pure maps `Refusals.FromBuildMismatch` / `FromEnterResponse` / `FromCreateResult` (Steam codes as numbers; no Steamworks types, so the console tests compile it) | `Refusals`, `RefusalKind`, `RefusalVersions` |
| SessionProgress.cs | the step a player is in: `SessionStep` (`Ready` = everything prepared, the launch waits for the start gate), the pure `SessionProgress.Step(StepInputs)` rule (`StartHeld` with a pending launch = `Ready`, after the save sync), the pure `StartGate.Open(isHost, held, requested, hostStep, latched)` (the host opens it with START GAME, a guest when the host's game is starting or running), `StepCodec` (the text published as member data `cf_step`: `step=ReceivingSave;pct=40`), `SaveProgressMeter` (bytes received over the sizes announced, `CurrentFile` / `ReceivedBytes` / `TotalBytes`, fed from the FILE_BEGIN / FILE_CHUNK frames; a file sent again under a new transfer id replaces its earlier transfer) | `SessionProgress.Step`, `StartGate.Open`, `StepCodec`, `SaveProgressMeter` |
| LobbyListing.cs | the lobby data keys `LobbyKeys` (`cf_app` marker `coop-launcher`, `cf_game`, `cf_proto`, `cf_ver`, `cf_opts`, `cf_join_pw`, `cf_host`, `cf_mod`, `cf_gbuild`, `cf_loc`, `cf_roster`, `cf_started`; no Steam types, so the console tests compile it), `RosterEntry` + `RosterCodec` (the roster text, one `name TAB step` line per member joined by LF, tabs and line breaks in a name become spaces, a malformed line is skipped) and `LobbyListing` (a lobby as the Lobbies tab, the friend cards and the joining screen show it; `Decode(data, lobbyId, ownerId, members, max, ping)` is pure and unit tested, equal by content) | `LobbyKeys`, `RosterCodec.Encode`, `Decode`, `LobbyListing.Decode` |
| LobbyDirectory.cs | finding other lobbies (Steam, loop thread): `Search` = lobby list filtered on `cf_app` and `cf_proto`, worldwide, 50 results (`CallResult<LobbyMatchList_t>`, an unanswered search is retried after 30 s), `ReadFriends` = `RequestLobbyData` for each friend lobby and its listing on `LobbyDataUpdate_t` (`FriendListing`), ping from `cf_loc` through `ParsePingLocationString` + `EstimatePingTimeFromLocalHost` (negative = unknown); the pure `Select(listings, knownGames, ownId)` keeps known games, drops the lobbies the local user hosts, one per lobby id, ping ascending with unknown last (unit tested) | `Search`, `ReadFriends`, `FriendListing`, `Results`, `Searching`, `Revision`, `Select` |
| LobbyCode.cs | a lobby id out of what a player pastes (digits, spaces, dashes, a label, a `steam://joinlobby/<app>/<lobby>/<host>` link); unit tested | `LobbyCode.TryParse` |
| FriendLobbies.cs | friends running the launcher with a lobby in their rich presence (Steam, loop thread) and the pure `Select` (known games, one row per lobby, sorted by name, at most 4); a row's `Listing` is attached by the app from `LobbyDirectory.ReadFriends` | `Read`, `Select` |
| SteamAvatars.cs | Steam avatars of the wanted users as RGBA (`AvatarImage`): looks them up, waits for `AvatarImageLoaded_t` / `PersonaStateChange_t` while Steam loads them, delivers a changed avatar again, `ResendAll` for a late window (loop thread only) | `Want`, `Pump`, `ResendAll`, `Loaded` |
| SaveSender.cs | host: paced FILE_BEGIN/CHUNK/END per new peer, resend until ACK ok; with a profile `filePattern` it sends every matching file after a FILE_MANIFEST 0x0044 (a missing save folder = empty manifest); a set the guest cannot take (over 255 names, a non-ASCII name, a file over `SaveReceiver.MaxFileBytes`) is not sent and logged once; `SendChanged` sends one later-written file (manifest again); a file the guest rejects (ACK not ok) is resent after `RetryAfterMs`, like an unanswered one, so a short lock on the guest does not use up the attempts; the clock is injectable for tests | `SendTo`, `SendChanged`, `OnAck`, `Pump` |
| SavePaths.cs | save folder templates: `{documents}`, `{steamid64}` (Steam's ActiveUser registry value); null when the template cannot be expanded here (no Steam user, unknown token) | `Expand` |
| SaveWatcher.cs | host: FileSystemWatcher on the save folder; reports a changed save once it has been quiet for 3 s (retry by touching it again); on the watcher's `Error` (buffer overflow) it is created again and every matching file is touched | `Touch`, `TakeSettled` |
| SaveReceiver.cs | guest: temp file (`TempSuffix` .part), sha256 check, move into session dir (or the profile's `guestSaveDir`), FILE_ACK; expects the profile's list or the host's manifest; after the first full set, or from the start when the game already runs (`gameRunning`), files go to `<sessionDir>/staging` and `PromoteStaged` moves them into the session dir; a disk error (locked file) drops that transfer, ACKs false and logs it, never throws; `MaxFileBytes` 64 MiB | `OnFrame`, `Complete`, `PromoteStaged`, `MaxFileBytes`, `TempSuffix` |
| FileMessages.cs | FILE_* payload builders/parsers (through `Framing.Reliable`); `IsWireName` and `MaxManifestNames` = what the manifest can carry | `Begin`, `Chunk`, `Manifest`, `TryParseBegin`, `TryParseManifest`, `IsWireName` |
| AdapterSettings.cs | `coop=` and the game's option keys in the adapter ini (one `SetKeys` writer; `WriteOptions` = the option keys alone, for a native-session game), session dir cleanup after copying its files (not staging or `.part`) to `<sessionDir's parent>/archive/<stamp>/` (`-2`, `-3` when the stamp exists): skipped when every file equals the newest archive's copy, newest kept always, at most `MaxArchives` 20, none older than `KeptArchiveDays` 30 (`ExpiredArchives`); a failed copy keeps the session folder | `EnableCoop(gameDir, profile, options)`, `WriteOptions`, `Reset`, `ExpiredArchives`, `ArchiveFolder`, `ArchiveRoot` |
| NativeJoin.cs | rendezvous games (SR2): writes `<game>\coop\sr2_join.txt` (`role` host/guest fixed when the session starts from the lobby owner of that moment, so a host leaving does not turn a guest into a host (the launcher then logs `The host left the lobby` once), `token` = the lobby id, `password` = `JoinSecret`, `name` = `PlayerName.ForLocal` when not empty) atomically once the lobby has both, again when it changes, deletes it on dispose; every 2 s reads the mod's `sr2_status.txt` (`state=<s> detail=<d>`) into member data `cf_status` (`MemberKey.GameStatus`); `Rebind` follows a held session to its new lobby; `Readable` turns a status line into the words the Players seats show | `Create`, `Pump`, `Rebind`, `Readable` |
| PlayerName.cs | the in-game name from the Steam persona names (pure; tests in `tools/sr2/player_name_test`, run with `dotnet run -c Release`): printable ASCII and Latin-1 kept, other code points `?`, controls dropped, trimmed, 15 characters; equal names among the lobby members get ` 2`, ` 3` in Steam id order within 15 | `Shape`, `ForLocal` |
| GameLauncher.cs | Steam game start, returns a `LaunchResult` (started, already running, unsupported game build, mod not installed (never installs), Steam start failed); `LaunchWithOptions(profile, gameDir, options)` writes the given options to the adapter ini first (rendezvous and native-session games); `IsRunning` disposes the processes it looks at and is the one process check | `Launch`, `LaunchWithOptions`, `IsRunning`, `LaunchResult` |
| GameBuilds.cs | the installed Steam build of a game (appmanifest beside its folder, else any library) against the profile's `supportedBuilds` (the adapter's addresses fit one executable); a known build outside a non-empty list is unsupported | `Installed`, `IsSupported`, `Describe` |
| GameWatch.cs | polled by the window while a session is open: the game exited or crashed (Relaunch is offered), has run 20 s without the adapter link (started outside the launcher), or the launcher started it (`AppStatus.LaunchedAtMs`) and no process appeared within 60 s (`NotStarted`); the decision is the pure `GameWatch.Next` (unit tested); only for a session with a lobby, so a native-session game is never watched; a rendezvous game is never Unlinked unless its session is bridged (SR2 transport=steam), then the adapter link is expected like a lobby game's; while the lobby is rebuilt (Rejoining, WaitingForHost) the held session keeps its link, so the game is not called unlinked then (`GameWatch.Linked`, unit tested); the main loop is untouched | `GameWatch.Update`, `Next`, `Linked`, `GameCondition` |
| ReportBundle.cs | one zip for sending: `report.txt` (launcher build, OS, per game folder / game build / mod state, included, truncated and skipped files with reasons), every `*.log` in the logs folder, `settings.json`, version.txt, per game the coop folder's adapter log/ini, peer logs, crash dumps and the profile's status file; never the join file; one unreadable file is skipped and listed; each log is cut to its last `MaxLogEntryBytes` 8 MiB, dumps and other files go whole (a dump cannot be read without its start) | `Create` |
| SteamLibrary.cs | game folder and installed build id from libraryfolders.vdf + appmanifest; a file that cannot be read is skipped (logged once), a parsed file is reused until its write time changes; pure parsers unescape `\\` | `FindGameDir`, `InstalledBuild(appId, gameDir)`, `ParseLibraryPaths`, `ParseManifest`, `ReadManifest` Custom Steam artwork: `CustomArtFiles`. |
| RepoPaths.cs | repo root / games dir discovery | |
| Log.cs | timestamped console log; `Written` event feeds the GUI log pane; `Event` = a player-facing line (also an Info line) for `SubscribeEvents` listeners with a 50 line history; `FileOnly` = a line for the file only (stack traces); the window also writes `AppData.LogFile`, and the logs of the previous ten runs are kept (`RotatePrevious`: `launcher.prev.log`, `launcher.prev2.log` ... `launcher.prev10.log`) | `Info`, `Event`, `FileOnly`, `Subscribe`, `SubscribeEvents`, `WriteToFile`, `RotatePrevious` |

## launcher/Web/ (the WebView2 window, namespace CoopLauncher.Web)

The page itself is `launcher/web/` (html, css, ES modules; embedded in the exe as `web/...`, served by `WebAssets`). The host knows the page only by the bridge of `launcher/web/SPEC.md` section 3: it posts `hello`, `state`, `log` and `event` messages and reads the commands of `UiCommands`.

| file | owns | key members |
|---|---|---|
| Web/WebRuntime.cs | the WebView2 runtime check (`MinRuntime` 130): its version, `--webview-check`, the TaskDialog with the download link when it is missing | `Version`, `UsableVersion`, `Check`, `EnsureOrExplain`, `Explain` |
| Web/WebShell.cs | the main window: a `ChromelessForm` holding one `WebSurface` under the top resize band (re-laid on maximize / restore), size / place / dark frame, minimize, close (confirmed through the page in a session), invite raise + flash, the 1 s clock, suspend when hidden or minimized, hand-over to the mini window, log and event feeds | `WebShell`, `BringForward`, `OnCommand`, `SyncMini` |
| Web/MiniShell.cs | the always-on-top mini window (kept hidden for reuse): shown once its page drew the first state, `shown` / `miniClose` handshake with the 400 ms close timeout, Alt+F4 = Expand | `Open`, `Close`, `CloseTimeoutMs` |
| Web/WebSurface.cs | one hardened WebView2 (shared environment, SPEC 4 settings, navigation / new window / download / permission blocking, origin check), coalesced and deduplicated posting with snapshots built at post time, suspend / resume, `ProcessFailed` handling | `StartAsync`, `RequestState`, `Post`, `Suspend`, `Resume`, `PageReady`, `CommandReceived`, `Failed` |
| Web/WebAssets.cs | `WebResourceRequested` for both hosts: page files from the manifest resources, images loaded in the background behind a deferral (Steam art, avatars, outfit figures and silhouettes, 404 for anything unknown) | `Attach`, `FigureCacheCapacity` |
| Web/ImageRoutes.cs | the two hosts (`app.coop.example`, `img.coop.example`) and the strict image URL patterns, built for the snapshot and parsed by the assets | `WebHosts`, `ImageRoutes.SteamUrl`, `AvatarUrl`, `FigureUrl`, `Parse`, `ImageRoute` |
| Web/UiController.cs, Web/UiController.Actions.cs | `MainForm`'s state and actions without controls (games and mod states, selected game, tab, intent, join target, view, popup, lobby filter, events, update flow, game watch, lobby search, mini); `Handle` runs a page command with the same rules and refuses what the state does not allow | `UiController`, `Changed`, `BuildMain`, `BuildMini`, `Handle`, `Run`, `Tick`, `NeedsClock` |
| Web/UiCommands.cs | the command names (`Cmd`), the field table, parsing and validation (under 4 KB, depth 3, exact fields), the origin rule, the rate-limited file-only drop log | `Cmd`, `UiCommands.Parse`, `Types`, `OriginAllowed`, `UiCommand`, `DropLog` |
| Web/ModRunView.cs | a game's mod action on show (`ModRunView`: kind install / uninstall + the last `ModProgress`) and its pure mapping (`ModRunViews.Dto`: the step row with text `Copying 12/50`, `N%`, bar, check on Done, the reason with Retry on Failed; `WhileRunning` locks Host, Join, the code and the mod button) | `ModRunViews.Dto`, `ModRunViews.WhileRunning` |
| Web/UiDtos.cs | the JSON records of the snapshot (camelCase, enums as camelCase text, ids as text; `modRun` on the home and on each settings game) and `UiJson` | `MainState`, `MiniState`, `UiJson` |
| Web/UiSnapshot.cs, Web/UiSnapshot.Session.cs | the pure mapping from the controller's state to the snapshot (`SnapshotInput` in, `MainState` / `MiniState` out) using the existing mappers; the transition runs from the last posted screen; card and select wire names (`Names`) | `UiSnapshot.MainPage`, `MiniPage`, `SnapshotInput`, `GameEntry` |
| Web/UiMessages.cs | the messages besides the snapshot (`hello`, `log`, `event`) and the `state` envelope with `seq` | `UiMessages.Hello`, `LogLines`, `Event`, `State` |
| Web/AvatarImages.cs, Web/Lru.cs | avatars encoded to PNG once on arrival (LRU of 128, a revision per avatar for the URL) and the small thread-safe LRU | `AvatarImages.Put`, `Revision`, `Png`, `Lru` |

## launcher/web (browse)

The WebView2 page (page spec: SPEC 2, 3, 6 of the launcher web UI). No build step; ES modules; text only through `textContent`.

| file | owns | key members |
|---|---|---|
| web/index.html, web/mini.html | the page shells: CSP meta, stylesheets, one module script (`main.js` / `mini-main.js`) | |
| web/css/tokens.css | the one `:root`: colours, fonts, every size and every motion variable | |
| web/css/base.css | reset, type, buttons (ghost, over-art and icon buttons share one hover / press language), field, select (`.select-value`, `.select-caret`; `.select-label` / `.select-arrow` belong to the character select), toggle, segments, icons, focus ring, `app-region` | `.btn`, `.field`, `.seg`, `.select`, `.toggle` |
| web/css/motion.css | every generic recipe as a class + keyframes (`m-*`), `html.paused`, `html.reduced` end states | `m-page-in`, `m-panel-in`, `m-modal-in`, `m-reveal`, `m-shake` |
| web/css/browse.css | top bar, home, lobbies, popups, settings, drawer | |
| web/js/main.js | boot, registry of every screen module, Esc rule, `page.rendered` after each applied state | `SCREEN_MODULES` |
| web/js/bridge.js | `COMMANDS` (SPEC 3.3), `connect`, `send`; WebView2 or the dev mock | `COMMANDS`, `connect`, `send` |
| web/js/dom.js | `h`, `svg`, `setText`, `setAttr`, `setVar`, `toggle`, keyed list with exit hook | `keyed`, `wasCreated` |
| web/js/motion.js | recipes as functions: `play`, `stagger`, `swapText`, `rollDigits`, `reel`, `shake`, `iconSwap`; reduced motion | `play`, `tokenMs` |
| web/js/icons.js | the fixed SVG glyphs | `icon(name, size)` |
| web/js/screen-host.js | one element per screen, the transition of the state (page, panelIn, panelOut) | `createScreenHost` |
| web/js/perf.js | debug overlay: frame p95, long tasks, state apply time | `createPerf` |
| web/js/segments.js, web/js/topbar.js | sliding-pill segmented control; the top bar | `createSegments` |
| web/js/notice.js | the notice row (also used by session screens) | `createNotice` |
| web/js/home.js, hero-layout.js, rail.js, friends.js, codefield.js | Home: the hero (the whole pane while nothing is below it, 296 px otherwise; the art's clip edge and everything hanging from the hero's bottom move with transforms, never layout per frame) with the action row (Host, Lobby code field of at least 20 digits, Join, cog), the Install / Characters buttons in the hero's top-right corner and the notice row, the games rail (sliding selection, Up / Down), FRIENDS PLAYING cards (keyed, staggered), the lobby code row (shake on `codeRefused`) | `home.create`, `createHeroLayout` (`run`, `arm`), `createRail`, `createFriends`, `createCodeField` |
| web/js/lobbies.js, dropdown.js, format.js | Lobbies tab (filter, game dropdown, 56 px rows, skeleton then reveal); the painted dropdown (menu-dropdown recipe, keyboard); `pingText` | `createDropdown`, `pingText` |
| web/js/modal.js, popup-lobby.js, popup-options.js, popup-header.js, confirm.js | modal layer (scrim + modal recipe), lobby popup (roster tints, facts, reel and digit roll), options popup (read-only for a guest), the shared art header, the close confirmation | `modal.create`, `createConfirm`, `createPopupHeader` |
| web/js/mod-run.js | the mod action's step row (spinner or check or cross, text, percent, tweened bar) and Retry; used by the home hero (over the art) and each settings game's line | `createModRun` |
| web/js/settings.js, toggle.js | Settings view: Launcher section on a label / control grid, one section per game (middle-ellipsis folder path with Browse, Reset, Uninstall, Archive in the same row, then the mod and game build items); the switch (light track, knob right = on) | `settings.create`, `createToggle` |
| web/js/drawer.js, art.js | footer strip with the newest event and the log (last 500 lines, rises by transform); images that hide on failure, avatars | `drawer.create`, `createImage`, `createAvatar` |
| web/js/joining.js, session.js, select.js, mini-main.js | stubs for the session builder | |
| web/dev/mock.js | dev transport: `?fixture=` / `?sequence=`, `&frame=`, `&reduced=1`, `&smoke=`, `window.__sent`, `html[data-ready]`; fixtures in `dev/fixtures`, sequences in `dev/sequences` (`{ keys, transitions?, events?, ms }`), smoke scripts in `dev/smoke`, a fixture's `smoke` / `events` run after its last state, art copy in `dev/art` (git-ignored) | `createTransport` |
| tools/launcher_web/render.py, perf.py | headless Edge screenshots of fixtures and sequences (throwaway profile and server), `--frames` (ms or `%` of a sequence), `--reduced`, `--smoke`, `--dump-dom`, `--prepare-art`; `perf.py` reads the perf overlay in real time over the DevTools socket (needs `websockets`) | |

## launcher/web (session)

Joining, hosting, lobby, mini window, character select and their components (briefs: concepts SPEC 6-9). Text only through `textContent`; every label, flag and state comes in the DTO.

| file | owns | key members |
|---|---|---|
| web/js/spinner.js | the matrix loader (orbit variant), small and large | `createSpinner(size)` |
| web/js/steps.js | the step list: glyphs (circle, spinner, check drawn in, cross + row shake), rolling trailing digits, bar tween, indeterminate bar, lines, Host/You pairs; keyed by row index | `createSteps(size)`, `setGlyph`, `fillList` |
| web/js/cards.js | player cards in wide / tall / mini / miniPortrait: portrait (figure, avatar, initial, open dots), state layers (tint, edge, glow, comet) cross-faded by `data-state`, shake and bloom on entering, occupant cross-fade, staggered `enter()`, clickable local card | `createCards(ctx, layout)`, `createPortrait`, `createAvatar`, `createImage`, `setImage` |
| web/js/joining.js | joining screen: backdrop, host header (text swap), large steps, Cancel / fix / Start again | `create` |
| web/js/session.js | hosting and lobby (two instances by `ctx.screen`): code + copy icon swap, hosting steps, lobby state line, options, events, buttons, notice row, PLAYERS reel, cards | `create`, `createCode` |
| web/js/select.js | character select S1 / S3: halves, outfit slide, character swap crossing, sliding dots, Left/Right/Tab keys | `create`, `createHalf` |
| web/js/mini.js, web/js/mini-main.js, web/mini.html | the mini window page: header, seats or the alert, event footer; `shown` / `miniClose` play the modal recipes | `createMini` |
| web/css/cards.css | spinner, step list, check / cross recipes, card layouts, state layers and loops (bloom, breathe, pulse, comet) | |
| web/css/session.css, web/css/mini.css | joining, hosting / lobby, select; the 360x220 mini page | |
| web/dev/fixtures/(home_mod_installing, settings_mod_verifying, mod_install_failed) | the mod action's step row at 40 percent on the home, verifying in a settings row, and a failure with its reason and Retry | |
| web/dev/fixtures/(joining, hosting, lobby, select, mini, cards_states, seq_*).js, web/dev/sequences/*.js, web/dev/smoke/select_keys.js, mini_expand.js | session fixtures, the eight sequences (hosting_to_lobby, step_done, step_failed, card_states, copy_swap, outfit_next, character_swap, mini_open), smoke scripts | |

## adapters/re0/ (C++20, x86, dinput8.dll proxy)

Build (from a VsDevCmd x86 shell): `cmake -S . -B build -G Ninja -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release && cmake --build build`. Output `build/dinput8.dll`, copied to `adapters/re0/dinput8.dll`. Unit tests (no game): every `build/*_test.exe` except `net_test` (needs a launcher) exits 0 and prints its pass line. Vendored MinHook in `third_party/minhook` (wrapped by src/hooks.cpp) and Dear ImGui (imgui core + `backends/imgui_impl_dx9`, MIT) in `third_party/imgui`.

| file | owns | key members |
|---|---|---|
| src/proxy.cpp, dinput8.def | DirectInput8Create forwarding, DllMain (spawns init thread, pins module); hands the created IDirectInput8 to virtual_keys | `DirectInput8Create`, `DllMain` |
| src/init.cpp | wait for code decryption, start subsystems, SEH boundary; `enableCoop` records `partner_mode` (`state_mode::configure`), registers the PAD_FRAME recorder only in input mode and calls `state_mode::enableAll` last in state mode | `initThread`, `shutdownAdapter` |
| src/protocol.h | loopback frame codec and message constants (ROOM_STATE, MENU_STATE, PARTY_REQUEST, PARTY_MODE, RESYNC_REQUEST 0x0114, FLOOR_SNAPSHOT 0x0115; APPEARANCE 0x0116 lives in appearance_rule.h) (mirrors Framing.cs); `bytesOf` turns a wire struct into a send payload | `proto::encodeFrame`, `bytesOf` |
| src/net_client.cpp | launcher link thread: HELLO, heartbeat, reconnect, queues; WELCOME may end with the local SteamID (`SessionSnapshot::localSteamId`, 0 when absent); an `onFrame` handler (SR2's tunnel) takes game frames on the link thread instead of the queue | `NetClient::send`, `poll`, `start` |
| src/state_sync.cpp | 60 Hz PLAYER_STATE send for the OWNED character (characterId, hp, senderIsHost, room) plus `focusedCharacterId` (the camera character, diagnostic), received-state log, peer join/leave toasts (a repeated PEER_UP for a slot, a new join serial, makes a guest call `resync::request("partner reconnected")`: the peer was unreachable for a while; no join toast) (the leave toast names the player; a guest whose host left gets "Host left: not saved" and `partner_hud::onHostLeft`) and frame dispatch (frames it does not know go to `frame_router::dispatch`, which hands state-mode types such as POSE_STATE / MOTION_EVENT to their registered handler, before the PLAYER_STATE handling; in input mode a state-block frame is dropped without the router search); a peer on the other `partner_mode` is named once (`state_sync: the peer runs partner_mode=...`, toast `Partner runs partner_mode=...`); in state mode the net tick also feeds `room_authority::onSession` | `state_sync::start`, `PlayerState` |
| src/game.h | RE0 addresses/offsets (players, enemies, `HitInfo`, `HitPoint`), SEH-safe reads and writes | `game::kTargetSelectors` (each class's own nearest-player selector: vtables or base update, function, target / distance / height fields, preference flag, Rebecca flag, `TargetPolicy`), `game::controlled`, `partner`, `readTransform`, `callThiscall`, `setThink` |
| src/game_tick.cpp | MinHook on `uPlayerBase::move`; runs registered callbacks (no limit, registered before install) once per frame before the controlled player moves, SEH-guarded: a faulting callback is reported (`crash_dump::reportCaught`), toasted "Sync stopped: <name>" and disabled until the next resync re-arms it (`rearmAfterResync`, called by join_sync when a snapshot is sent or applied); `onGameThread` (the thread of the last tick; the recorders that fill game-thread-only buffers from `cAnimControl::set` (`pose_game`'s observers) and `sEffect::create` (`weapon_fx_capture::add`) ignore calls from other threads); move scopes (`addMoveScope`, at most 4, entered in registration order and exited in reverse, safe to add while live; at runtime puppet registers first, input_redirect after `install`; a scope that raises an exception is reported like a callback, toasted and disabled, enter and exit, until the next resync) and guarded post-move hooks (`addPostMove(name, fn)`, a list like the callbacks: state_correction "post_move", pose_capture); every callback, move scope (optional `name`, only picks the SLOW_FRAME column) and post-move step runs inside a `slow_frame::Scope`, and each tick opens a frame with `slow_frame::beginFrame` | `game_tick::addCallback`, `rearmAfterResync`, `addMoveScope`, `addPostMove`, `install` |
| src/hooks.cpp | shared MinHook wrapper: one init, retried install (game settling after SteamStub), status logging, `remove` (inside a batch it takes back the queued enable and drops the hook from the batch's list, so a failed batch enable's one-by-one pass does not turn it on again; unit tested by SR2's `tests/hooks_batch_test.cpp`) | `hooks::install`, `remove` |
| src/game_state.cpp | read-only SEH-guarded views: door transition active, menu open, current room (stage << 8 \| room; `kRoomLoading` while loading), room phase, `uiPausesWorld` (menu, map, message, save screens), `playing` (Main, no door, no menu: peer doors, placements and party commands wait for it), character in the loaded room (its record is the loaded one and its in-room flag is set) | `game_state::doorActive`, `menuOpen`, `currentRoom`, `roomPhase`, `inCurrentRoom` |
| src/door_phase.h | pure door phase predicate (`door_phase::running`) | `door_phase::running` |
| src/door_travel.cpp | where the peer is: ROOM_STATE (0x0104, 8 bytes) {loaded scene id, partner in room, enemy claim, door target (while the room phase is DoorLoad), door flags shared / ready} on every arrival, at every door start (from whichever thread sees it, so the peer knows where the door leads) and every 2 s; `peerReport`; `peerPlace` (Here / Elsewhere / Unknown) for every room decision; `enemyAuthority` (changes logged): the first machine in a room keeps its enemies when the other walks in (host on a tie); the door start is latched level-triggered from the game tick and the net thread; arrival forces a position check and calls `floor_items_sync::onArrival`; `announce` sends ROOM_STATE at once; "Room desync" toast after 3 s of mismatch, `resync::request` every 10 s while it lasts (3 times); in state mode `enemyAuthority` answers from `room_authority::runsHere(scene::current())` and the claim / runsEnemies path is not used | `door_travel::enable`, `onFrame`, `onNetTick`, `announce`, `peerReport`, `peerPlace`, `enemyAuthority`; in state mode no desync toast or automatic resync (ROOM_MOVE and the pose room self-heal in `room_move` replace them) |
| src/room_gate.cpp, src/room_gate_rule.h | door barrier: hooks the door's finish check 0x551c70 (only caller: the door's last phase; finishing ends room phase DoorLoad and starts the room); for a door both machines play (`door_sync::sharedDoor`) it reports the door ready (ROOM_STATE flag) and answers "not yet" while the peer's same door is not ready, so the faster machine's door stays on its last frame and both rooms start their enemies together; released when the peer's door is ready, it turns away, the link drops or after 5 s; "Waiting for partner" only past 2 s; the pure rule (unit tested) decides hold and release; in state mode (phase 8) `install` hooks nothing and logs `room_gate: off in state mode`: no door ever waits, the second arrival's enemies are observers of the first one's | `room_gate::install`, `doorReady`, `room_gate_rule::holdsAtFinish`, `check` |
| src/door_sync.cpp | doors on both machines: hooks sDoorLoad::start (0x552b50, door animation then room change); the focused character's owner runs it and sends DOOR_CHANGE 0x010B, the other machine suppresses its own call, focuses the character that went through and runs the peer's on the game tick; a local player whose character is the partner acts on doors/triggers through the act-on-trigger check (0x564070); a peer's door runs without moving the camera; F8 doors sent/run/blocked; remembers each character's last door (`lastDoor`, a carried partner included) and can `queue` one (join travel, `bothTravel`); hands peer doors to split_rooms when it takes them ; `sharedDoor`: the running door is played on both machines (a local door sent while `split_rooms::travelsTogether`, or the peer's door run here; not a join teleport); a door from a room script that left the partner behind is sent with DOOR_CHANGE flag `kDoorAlone` and the peer never goes along (`setScriptDoorAlone`, set by event_sync) | `door_sync::enable`, `onFrame`, `uninstall`, `queue`, `run`, `sharedDoor`; state mode (phase 10): the local door asks the partner's owner to follow (`door_follow::onLocalDoorStart`) instead of sending DOOR_CHANGE, `sharedDoor` is false (no barrier), a received DOOR_CHANGE is ignored, `runFollow` starts a followed door for the own character through the local path (`split_rooms::beforeLocalDoor`, then `run`) |
| src/scene.cpp | the engine's room records (sSceneInfo, up to 4: the loaded room plus dormant rooms a character is in): scene id of a character or the loaded room, and `move` = the door carry's own calls for one character (find or load the room's record 0x61e0f0, place on the entry spot 0x61ed50, assign 0x619e30, unit registry 0x411570, release an empty dormant record 0x61dde0), SEH-guarded; the load serial (`load_serial.h`) is fed from the loaded record once per game tick in state mode and logs `scene: load serial N (scene 0x..)`; `loadedRecord` = the loaded room record (the enemy create's first argument) | `scene::of`, `current`, `move`, `loadSerial`, `observeLoad` | `scene::of`, `current`, `move`, `loadedRecord`; `move` takes an optional entry spot mode (the owner's, from ROOM_MOVE; without it `spot_rule` chooses) and `placeBehind` puts a character on the follower spot of an entry in its own record (state mode follower doors); `kSceneIdLimit` is the one bound for scene ids; `unitIndex` (the unit registry's lookup by scene, class, kind and n-th, 0x410070) and `unitObject` (0x51fed0) are the one place a unit becomes an object (event_body, prop_game) |
| src/event_place.cpp | a local cutscene or scripted event (EventDemo, Event, Movie) that moved the peer's character more than 50 units or into another room sends CHARACTER_PLACE (0x0113) to the owner, which places its own character there (into another room through `scene::move` and the game's switch); state_correction pauses during local events; in state mode `mark` / `reportMoved` bracket a script op that wrote the copy of the peer's character (SubMove for an owner that has no thread, climb type 7) and send CHARACTER_PLACE when the copy moved more than 50 units or into another room (`event_place: a script op moved .. to scene 0x.., sent to its owner`) | `event_place::onFrame`, `enable`, `mark`, `reportMoved` |
| src/split_rooms.cpp | players in different rooms and independent play (always on): a peer door that is not a TEAM door together moves the peer's character straight into its new room record (`scene::move`), no screen change here (into the loaded room it appears at the door, out of it it leaves, between other rooms its dormant record follows); `beforeLocalDoor` (a local door into a room whose dormant record holds only the peer's character: the character steps into the loaded room, the record is freed, the door loads the room fresh so enemies and floor items spawn) and `onArrival` (puts it back), `apart` = peer elsewhere or the own character outside the loaded room, `independent` = apart or LEAVE_BEHIND (own camera on each machine), enemy authority from door_travel ; `travelsTogether` = together and TEAM (a door is played on both machines); in state mode `localEnemyAuthority` answers from `room_authority::runsHere` (readers: `enemy_damage_hook`, `enemy_state`, `enemy_target`) | `split_rooms::takeOver`, `beforeLocalDoor`, `onArrival`, `apart`, `travelsTogether`, `independent`, `localEnemyAuthority`, `enable` |
| src/event_sync.cpp, src/event_rule.h | room script events on both machines (docs/re/RE0_EVENT_SYNC.md): hooks sEventScript's trigger start 0x57f800 (shared triggers refused unless this machine is the room's authority; local ones fire where they were met), the opcode dispatch 0x57ce80 and the script update 0x5835a0. A thread's leader (the owner of the character the script treats as the player) runs it natively and sends EVENT_START (0x0130) and each finished op as EVENT_STEPS (0x0131); the follower starts the same thread, runs an op only after the leader finished it, takes the leader's branch, skips leader-only ops (messages, close-ups, item screens, doors, equips) and waits, and runs the rest with the subject as the controlled character. CharChange* passes the lead to the other player (no camera switch); TraceOff/TraceOn set the host's party mode and make the thread's doors leave the partner behind; late arrivals jump to the leader's pc, a disconnect releases followers. Pure rules (trigger classes, op roles, follow decisions, who leads) unit tested; F8 events fired/followed/refused/dropped | `event_sync::enable`, `onFrame`, `event_rule::classify`, `roleOf`, `beforeOp`, `afterRun`, `leadsHere`, `startFate`; `leadsAny` (a thread this machine leads is running) is what `door_follow` asks before it accepts a door; state mode (phase 11): ops that write one character's body (`OpRole::Body`: position, motion, think, climb, attachment, reset, game over) run only where that character is owned, `runAsSubject` asks `event_body::plan` and steps over a puppet's op (`stepOverBody`) or runs it with the puppet's slot of sPlayer reading 0 (PlayerEventStart); a Demo_Start op tells `demo_skip` which demo plays and who leads it; `event_rule::targetOf`, `bodyRun`, `runFor`, `placesPartner` are the pure rules; state mode (phase 13): conditions 4 / 5 naming an obstacle unit (p2 0..4) classify as `TriggerKind::Obstacle` and fire on the box's pusher (`prop_sync::triggerOwner` gives `PropOwner` Mine / Theirs / Unknown; Unknown falls back to the room authority), `classify(type, p2, obstaclesByPusher)` and `mayFire(kind, peerInRoom, roomAuthority, owner)`; phase 16: `followsAny` (this machine follows a thread the peer leads, what the save gate asks) and `leadsAny` (what `door_follow` asks); both mean a BUSY thread (review fix): `event_rule::Activity` per thread slot, noted at every dispatch, busy while the thread is at (or within `kBusyHoldMs`, 2 s, of) a LeaderOnly, Body or Switch op, so a thread that only polls never counts |
| src/flag_sync.cpp | story flags (sFlagManager 0xdcc014 +0x20, 0x11c bytes) kept equal: every 6 frames each machine sends changed words as set/clear masks (FLAG_DIFF 0x010C); received non-enemy changes go through the game's setter 0x59bd50 (per changed bit, so effect/act/display/BGM side effects run on the peer; BGM refresh 0x643490 after a BGM change); enemy bits are written raw (docs/re/RE0_FLAGS.md); changes with a word outside the bitset are dropped in onFrame; all received changes are adopted as known (no echo); each sent and received bit is logged with its family (story, enemy, effect, act, display, bgm); F8 flag words sent/applied; state mode: a local load of a player save (`onLocalLoad`) holds the tracker still until the loaded room is in (`flag_load_hold_rule::LoadHold`, room load serial, capped) and re-baselines, and a guest diffs only while `join_sync::caughtUp()` (sync review F1) | `flag_sync::enable`, `onFrame`, `applyIncoming`, `logChanges`, `onLocalLoad` |
| src/join_sync.cpp | joining a game in progress: the loaded guest sends SNAPSHOT_REQUEST 0x010D (every 3 s until answered); the host answers JOIN_SNAPSHOT 0x010E (per character: scene, last door, position; both inventories; flags); the guest brings its own character into its loaded room first (game switch), applies flags and inventories, takes its own character through its last door when it is elsewhere, then moves the host's character into its room (`scene::move`) and places its own; `caughtUp` holds back the guest's PLAYER_STATE and inventory sends until then; in state mode a sent / applied snapshot also resends this machine's room claims (`room_authority::resendClaims`) | `join_sync::enable`, `onFrame`, `caughtUp`, `requestResync` (apply a new snapshot like the first); the host also sends the floor journal after the snapshot; state mode (phase 16): the host sends JOIN_EXTRAS right after JOIN_SNAPSHOT (`join_extras::send`, in place of the claims resend), `finish` does not move the host's character (ROOM_MOVE and the pose room self-heal place the puppet), `placed()` = applied and standing (what lets the pose stream start), `caughtUp()` = the SESSION_READY echo has arrived (`session_ready::echoed`); a loaded-again game or a dropped link resets the handshake and the held extras (`forgetPlacement`) |
| src/session_slot.cpp | session save slot: hooks the save manager's save (0x6134c0, and 0x613500 save by copy) and load (0x613390) requests; the host remembers its player slot (0..19) and announces {slot, room phase} (SAVE_SLOT 0x010F); on a guest every player-slot load becomes the host's slot; a save from the in-game save screen during a session goes to the co-op slot (19, shown as 20) so solo saves are never overwritten (the system data save, slot 0x15, and the title's boot-time load pass through); a guest's save (`guestSession`: playing the session copy, also after the host left) is refused with a toast (`session_rule::saveRoute`; the save screen retries a refused request every frame); every accepted save or load clears the floor journal | `session_slot::enable`, `onFrame`, `onNetTick`, `current`, `guestSession`, `hostInGame`; state mode (phase 16): a host save with a partner waits until the partner stands in plain gameplay (`save_gate::check`; the request is refused and the log says `session_slot: save waits for plain gameplay (reason)`, F8 `saves waited`; the wait is bounded, see save_gate_rule.h); a save goes to `onSave` (`floor_items_sync::onSave`), a load of a player save to `onLoad`: new world, so `floor_items_sync::onLoad`, `item_ledger::resetTake`, `flag_sync::onLocalLoad` and `world_epoch::onLocalLoad` (sync review F1 to F3) |
| src/auto_join.cpp | net thread: a guest at game over (always) or outside gameplay (boot, title, load list; only with `auto_join=1`) with the host in game gets virtual Enter every 2.5 s and a muted keyboard until a character is controlled; waits muted while the host is not in game (60 s at most, then the keyboard is the guest's); the decision is `session_rule::joinMode` | `auto_join::onNetTick` |
| src/partner_status.cpp | pure text of the partner status line (unit tested): name, condition Fine / Caution / Danger from hp against the highest hp seen (floor 150), same room or `room 0x..`, `in menu`; "Host left / not saved" | `partner_status::text`, `condition` |
| src/partner_hud.cpp | net thread: gathers the status line, on host and guest alike, from the session (the other peer), its own copy of the partner's latest PLAYER_STATE (`onFrame`), `door_travel::peerPlace` and `menu_mirror::peerMenuOpen`, hands it to `debug_stats::setPartnerLine` on change; remembers a host that left | `partner_hud::onNetTick`, `onPeerJoined`, `onHostLeft` |
| src/resync.cpp | resync on demand: F9 (command_input), `coop\resync_now.txt` (polled every second, deleted when taken) and `request` (door_travel's persisting desync): a guest asks for a new join snapshot (`join_sync::requestResync`), a host sends RESYNC_REQUEST 0x0114 and the guest does | `resync::request`, `onFrame`, `onNetTick` |
| src/session_rule.h | pure session-flow rules (unit tested): where a save request goes (`saveRoute`: pass, co-op slot, refuse for a guest) and what the automatic join does (`joinMode`: idle, waiting, confirming) | `session_rule::saveRoute`, `joinMode` |
| src/virtual_keys.cpp | virtual keyboard on DirectInput: the proxy hands over IDirectInput8, CreateDevice is hooked, the keyboard's GetDeviceState records the real keys of each read (`realKeyDown`, all up when the read fails) and gets tapped keys, optional muting and two hidden keys | `virtual_keys::onDirectInput`, `tap`, `realKeyDown`, `setRealKeyboardMuted`, `setHiddenKeys` |
| src/jitter_target.h | adaptive remote-pad buffer target (2..8 frames: +1 per underrun, -1 after ~10 s calm; skip beyond target + 6), unit tested | `JitterTarget` |
| src/pad_buffer.h | remote pad frames between arrival and replay: ordered replay once the JitterTarget depth is reached, underrun, skip-ahead, cap 64 (shared by net_pad and trace_replay, unit tested) | `PadBuffer` |
| src/crash_dump.cpp | unhandled-exception filter: writes coop\crash-<date>-<time>.dmp (MiniDumpWriteDump) and a log line, then chains to the previous filter; installed at start and again after decryption. `reportCaught` is the __except filter for guarded code: logs code, address (raw = game VA, and module+offset) and the address an access violation touched, and dumps the first caught exception of the session to coop\caught-<date>-<time>.dmp | `crash_dump::install`, `reportCaught` |
| src/net_trace.cpp | opt-in (`net_trace=1`) recording of received PAD_FRAME and PLAYER_STATE packets with arrival ms to coop/net_trace.bin | `net_trace::enable`, `record`, `TraceRecord` |
| tools/trace_replay.cpp | offline: replays a net_trace.bin (or a synthetic link with latency/jitter/loss) through PadBuffer at 60 fps; reports underruns, skips, buffer delay and PLAYER_STATE gaps | `main` |
| src/flag_diff.h | pure word diff/apply for flag_sync (unit tested) | `flag_diff::diff`, `apply` |
| src/flag_family.h | pure classifier for story flag bits: story, enemy (0x312+n), effect (0x412+n), act (0x612+n), display (0x712+n), bgm (0x812+n), unmapped; names the family in logs (unit tested). The per-flag table is docs/re/RE0_FLAGS.md | `flag_family::classify`, `name` |
| src/room_phase.h | room phase ids and names (sRoomControl +0xb8 manager) and which phases pause the world | `room_phase::name`, `pausesWorld` |
| src/phase_watch.cpp | net thread: logs every room phase change by name (`phase: Main -> EventDemo`), F8 room phase | `phase_watch::onNetTick` |
| src/menu_mirror.cpp | MENU_STATE (0x0105, {open, room phase}) sent from the net thread on menu open/close/phase change and every 2 s while open; hooks `sUnit::updateAll` 0x727b50 and skips it while a peer's screen holds the world (`menu_hold_rule`: reading screens and cutscenes until closed, menus 20 s) and ours is closed; `peerMenuOpen` for the status line; hooks sSubMenu::open (0x5d9030): a player whose character is the partner gets the focus moved to it locally while the menu is open (inventory perspective), given back on close; snapshots inventories for exchange detection | `menu_mirror::enable`, `onNetTick`, `onFrame`, `peerMenuOpen` |

| src/enemy_net.cpp | the owner's enemy events in one ordered queue (oldest dropped past 256): hits, one outcome on both screens (the owner runs every hit after recording the enemy's HP and the game's random state and sends both with the HP after; the other machine sets that HP, swaps the random state in, runs the same damage function, restores its own, and takes the owner's HP after if the replay ended elsewhere; the reaction the owner's damage function set (a record change during the call) is started on the follower when its enemy does not show it (`enemy_decision::onOwnerOutcome`); a request is applied and an applied hit replayed whoever believes it owns the room; damage no player dealt travels as its outcome: HP after and reaction), and decisions (to `enemy_decision::offer`); events of another room than the loaded one, or hits on another class in the slot, are dropped (pool slots are reused per room); every drop is logged; `runAsOwner` tells `enemy_target::noteAttacker` which character hit the enemy; in state mode a HIT_APPLIED for an observed enemy (enemy_observer::replicates: shown from a stream or held under a lease) is not replayed: its HP, reaction and death come from the simulator's stream; in state mode (phase 8) `routeOwnHit` sends a local player's hit to `enemy_observer::simulatorOf` (log `enemy_net: hit for slot N sent to slot M`), a HIT_REQUEST is applied only by the machine that simulates the enemy, otherwise sent on once with `forwarded` set (`hit for slot N forwarded to slot M`) or dropped (`dropped (nobody simulates it | forwarded twice)`); F8 `enemy hits forwarded/unrouted` | `enemy_net::applyAsOwner`, `requestHit`, `sendDecision`, `onFrame`, `routeOwnHit` |

| src/enemy_decision.cpp | one decision maker per enemy, the room's enemy owner; the follower takes each decision where its own engine decides, through the class's own setAction (vtable slot 63): base family (15 vtables, think 0x41db20 and setAction 0x4cc670 MinHooked): the owner's think reports each record it chose (ENEMY_DECISION with pose and class), the follower's think runs with its own setAction calls dropped and the owner's newest decision is stored instead; executor classes (18 vtables, the 14 executors in game.h `kEnemyExecutorFunctions` MinHooked, one detour each): a record whose state or id changed across one executor call is an action boundary, the owner reports it, and at the follower's own boundary the owner's action of the same state replaces the local choice from its first step; the pose is set to the owner's decision pose when over 40 off; a decision for another class is dropped; waiting decisions per slot and room, superseded by an owner's damage outcome, whose reaction it also starts when this enemy does not show it; in state mode an observed enemy's think step and executor run natively (nothing replaced, the stream drives it); the simulator still reports; `startAction` starts an action through the class's own setAction (the enemy hand-over uses it) | `enemy_decision::install`, `offer`, `seed`, `reset`, `onOwnerOutcome`, `startAction` |

| src/enemy_observer_send_rule.h | pure (unit tested): what the simulator sends per pool slot and 20 Hz round: `SendPlanner::next` answers Pose (alive), Terminal (one reliable entry at the death, also for an enemy first seen dead), Corpse (a dead enemy once a second, staggered by slot), Removed (one reliable entry when the unit left or another took the slot) or Skip | `enemy_observer_send_rule::SendPlanner`, `kPoseIntervalMs` |
| src/enemy_observer_game.cpp | the engine side of the enemy observers, every access exception-safe: `read` fills an ENEMY_POSE entry from an enemy (HP +0x1030, position, rotation, motion layers, record +0x67a4, hit attributes +0x16ec, +0x6a0c), `write` puts a shown sample into it (setHP only when HP differs, record words only past setup, attributes without touching the upper bits), `recordState`, and the CharPause bit (sGameInfo +0x50 bit 0) hold / release as found | `enemy_observer_game::read`, `write`, `recordState`, `holdCharPause`, `releaseCharPause` |
| src/enemy_observer_capture.cpp | simulator side (state mode): a `cAnimControl::set` observer queues the requests of Observer-class pool enemies, flushed once a frame as ENEMY_MOTION; a 20 Hz round (`pose_capture_rule::SampleTimer` with 50 ms) sends ENEMY_POSE through `state_net` (living and repeated corpses unreliable, deaths and removals reliable); phase 9: it streams the room's enemies except those given away (`enemy_lease_rule::streams`), or, on a machine that only holds leases, just those, flagged leased with the token; logs `enemy_observer: sending N enemies, B B/s` once a second; `entryFor` builds the pose entry of one pool enemy (also used by `enemy_observer::snapshot`) | `enemy_observer_capture::enable`, `tick(scene, epoch, simulatesRoom)`, `stop`, `entryFor` |
| src/enemy_observer.cpp | receiver side (state mode, design STATE_PLAN 1.3): when the peer runs the loaded room, `onFrame` (net thread) queues the batches registered with the frame router (Any; checked per batch against `room_authority::holderOf`), a game tick callback refreshes the world (observing / simulating, the holder's `puppet::clockFor` clock, the pool, each slot's class and `enemy_spawn::originOf`), drains the batches into per-slot `SlotTimeline`s and motion queues (an entry for another class or spawn is refused) and calls the simulator's capture; a MinHook on the unit move 0x526b30 is a pass-through except for an observed enemy past construction: it writes the shown sample, replays due motions through the original `cAnimControl::set` (bank check 0x73e650), seeks layer 0 by `puppet_rule::SeekTracker`, and holds the CharPause bit around the original move (the engine then runs only the motion update, root cancel and joint / hit-shape updates); stale, missing or mismatched data holds, nothing is decided; a `removed` sample hands the unit back to its own code; `replicates(enemy)` is what the decision, alignment and hit-replay paths of the input-mode design ask (an enemy a stream shows, or one simulated here under a lease); phase 8: `simulatorOf(slot)` (the holder of the lease on it, else the room authority), hand-over when this machine takes over the room it was observing (`handOver`: every scoped enemy leaves the CharPause path and carries on from its shown record, an action restarts at step 0 through the class's setAction, a think state or a corpse is left to the class; while a lost room has no simulator the enemies stay held for up to 2 s), creation on demand for a living stream enemy whose slot is empty here (paced 4 a frame, one a second per slot), and the phase 15 API `freeze` / `snapshot` / `startAt`; phase 9: a slot's role comes from `enemy_lease` (Away: shown from the lease holder's stream and clock although this machine runs the room; Held: simulated natively, no scope), a leased pose is taken only through `enemy_lease::acceptPose`, `resume` carries a given-away enemy on from the holder's final state, the leases are released when the room's role restarts | `enemy_observer::enable`, `replicates`, `uninstall`, `simulatorOf`, `freeze`, `snapshot`, `resume`, `startAt` |
