## tools/re0/fake_peer/ (Python, stdlib only; `python tools\re0\fake_peer <command>`)

One-PC fake peer for RE0 state mode: plays the second machine (guest with the game as host, or host with the game as guest) on the same PC, speaks the whole state protocol, emulates the peer half of every flow, and checks the game's half from `adapter.log`. Commands: `list`, `run <scenario> [--seat guest|host] [--scratch] ...`, `gates <scenario>`, `library build`, `golden`, `selftest [--smoke]`. Runs write `<game>\coop\fake_peer\<stamp>-<scenario>\` (frames.jsonl, launcher logs, adapter segment, report.txt / report.json). Design, coverage and limits: `docs/RE0_NOTES.md` "State mode: one-PC fake peer"; commands per scenario: `adapters/re0/README.md` "One-PC state tests with the fake peer".

| file | owns | key members |
|---|---|---|
| __main__.py, cli.py | the command line (puts `tools
e0` on `sys.path` once) | `main`, `build_parser`, `options_from` |
| wire.py | the one table of every message id (`Msg`), payload layout (`Layout`: `PoseState`, `EnemyPoseEntry`, `AuthMessage`, ...), the frame codec, smallest-three quaternions, the variable-size payloads (extras, enemy batches, flags, boss, floor snapshot, WEAPON_FX position shift) | `Layout.pack/unpack/make`, `encode_frame`, `read_frame`, `pack_extras`, `shift_weapon_fx_x` |
| wire_check.py | compares wire.py with the adapter headers (struct sizes, message ids, limits), reads `kEnemyVtables` / `kBossVtables` from `game.h`, checks that every gate's log anchor is still in the adapter / launcher sources | `check_all`, `vtables`, `check_anchors` |
| golden.py, tests/golden_frames.bin | one accepted and at least one refused sample per state type; read by `adapters/re0/tests/fake_peer_wire_test.cpp` | `samples`, `write`, `is_current` |
| link.py | transports (`BridgeLink` to a launcher bridge, `StandinLink` as the game's own launcher), heartbeats, the shaped send and receive paths (both directions go through the `Shaper`), the receive `Dispatcher` and the `Module` base | `Link.send`, `Link.pump`, `StandinLink.new_epoch/peer_down` |
| shaper.py, impair.py, profiles.json | the seeded link model: `Impairment` (delay, jitter, burst loss as Gilbert-Elliott, reorder, duplicates, bandwidth with tail drop; ported from coop-anything's tools/chaos, MIT, notice in the header), the named profiles `lan` to `hell`, and the two-direction `Shaper` (reliable frames get delay and stall only, unreliable the full model; `cut` and `stall` windows, one-way or both) | `Impairment.decide`, `make_params`, `Shaper.cut/stall/configure`, `Direction.plan`, `TimedQueue` |
| launchers.py | finds and spawns the two CLI launchers (local transport), their stdout files, stops only those | `Launchers`, `find_launcher`, `Ports` |
| peer.py | the fake machine: identity, place, sender clock, sequence counters, the periodic PLAYER_STATE / ROOM_STATE / host status; `RemoteView` = what the game last said | `Peer.place_at`, `Peer.next`, `RemoteView.in_room` |
| sources.py | pose sources: echo (the game's streams re-sent later and beside it), replay, stand, held, silent | `Sources.use`, `emit`, `send_pose` |
| session.py | SNAPSHOT_REQUEST / JOIN_SNAPSHOT / FLOOR_SNAPSHOT / JOIN_EXTRAS / SESSION_READY / RESYNC_REQUEST, both seats; the host's ready judge | `SessionFlows`, `judge_ready` |
| rooms.py | ROOM_MOVE / ROOM_STATE moves, DOOR_FOLLOW answers (commit, busy, other room, none), the fake's own doors from the library, door race, self-heal probe | `Rooms.move`, `run_door`, `probe_self_heal` |
| authority.py | the room authority mirror (DS2 `AuthorityTable` rules in Python) and the fake's claim / release / decline actions (`normal`, `tie`, `first`, `force`) | `AuthorityTable`, `RoomAuthority.reconcile` |
| enemies.py | observer validation of ENEMY_POSE / ENEMY_MOTION, the shooter (HIT_REQUEST from a captured one), the simulator by replay with a damage model, wrong-binding probes | `Enemies.on_pose`, `shoot`, `start_replay`, `probe` |
| contact.py | PLAYER_HIT, ENEMY_LEASE, ENEMY_LEASE_END: the victim seat (lease modes) and the authority seat (hit, grab, the holder-pose acceptance rule) | `Contact.hit`, `grab`, `accept_holder_pose` |
| world.py | the host item ledger, the flag sequencer (propose, commit, correct, gap, cross) and the push block leases, both seats | `Ledger`, `Items`, `Flags`, `Props` |
| boss.py | BOSS_HANDOVER, both seats (new authority: ready, hold, outcome; old authority: door) | `Boss.door`, `kill` |
| story.py | PLAYER_DIED, the save-gate states (in a door, busy think), DEMO_SKIP_REQUEST / DEMO_SKIP | `Story.die`, `in_door`, `busy_think` |
| library.py | the frame journal (`frames.jsonl`) and the library of recordings (doors, pose / enemy clips, hit requests, lease grants, floor items, boxes) | `Journal`, `Library.ingest`, `build` |
| logwatch.py | live tails of `adapter.log` and the launcher stdout files, crash dump watch, `LogStore.wait` | `FileTail`, `LogStore`, `DumpWatch` |
| gates.py | the assertion layer: matchers (`Line`, `NoneWithin`, `Order`, `Wire`, `Agree`, `SumEquals`, `Latency`), `Gate.judge`, the global forbids, `Report` | `Gate`, `GLOBAL_FORBIDS`, `Report` |
| gamedir.py | RE0's folder from Steam's `libraryfolders.vdf` and the appmanifest (two reads, no drive sweep) | `find_game_dir` |
| runner.py | `Context` (the scenario API: `step`, `wait_log`, `wait_frame`, `source`, `shape`, `mark`, `require`, `manual`), needs check, threads, `run_scenario` | `Context`, `check_needs`, `run_scenario` |
| scenarios/*.py | one per test plan row: `t1_input`, `t2_echo`, `t4_join`, `t5_observe`, `t5_observer`, `t6_authority`, `t7_flags`, `t8_contact`, `t8_victim`, `t9_rooms`, `t10_props`, `t11_boss`, `t12_items`, `t13_death_save`, `t14_link`, `record`; each has `gates(seat)` and `run(ctx)`; `common.py` = shared helpers | `scenarios.REGISTRY` |
| selftest.py, smoke.py | `selftest`: wire check, golden freshness, anchors, unit tests; `--smoke`: two fakes against each other through two real launchers (ports 28960 / 28961 / 28962 / 28970), one round of every flow | `run` |
| tests/test_*.py, tests/support.py | unit tests (`unittest`): codec, authority rules, ledger / flag / prop mirrors, gate engine on synthetic logs, log tail, the link model and shaper (`test_impair.py`), transports on sockets, library, modules on a recording link, runner and CLI | `make_context`, `deliver` |

## adapters/ds2/ (C++20, x64, version.dll proxy, DEATH STRANDING 2)

Build (from a VsDevCmd `-arch=amd64` shell): `cmake -S . -B build -G Ninja -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release && cmake --build build`. Output `build/version.dll`, copied to `adapters/ds2/version.dll`. Unit tests (no game): `ctest --test-dir build`. Shares the launcher link, logging, crash dumps, toasts, position blending, the MinHook wrapper and the vendored MinHook / Dear ImGui (now with `backends/imgui_impl_dx12`) with `adapters/re0` (compiled from `../re0/src` and `../re0/third_party`). Engine-specific code sits behind `src/game.h`; `src/decima/` holds engine types shared by every Decima game, `src/ds2/` this game's build.

| file | owns | key members |
|---|---|---|
| src/proxy.cpp | the 17 version.dll exports forwarded to the system DLL by linker directives; DllMain (only inside DS2.exe: the crash reporter loads the DLL too) installs the session-save redirect, pins the module and starts the init thread | `DllMain`, `hostIsGame` |
| src/init.cpp | start-up: crash dumps, config, the build guard (unsupported exe: toast-only overlay, no hooks), DX12 overlay hooks, the remote body's engine hooks when enabled, input filter, interaction watch, waits for the player to exist, the simulation-thread tick (vehicles), then the launcher link | `initThread`, `simulationTick` |
| src/build_guard.cpp | compares DS2.exe's PE TimeDateStamp and SizeOfImage with the build the fixed addresses were taken from (Steam 23923251); a mismatch installs no hooks | `build_guard::checkRunningGame`, `identityOf`, `supported`, `kSupported` |
| src/partner_status.cpp | the PLAYER_STATE status word: health byte and dead/down/loading/driving/menu flags, its texts, the died / back transition and `away` (dead or loading parks the partner's body) (unit tested) | `partner_status::encode`, `decode`, `stateText`, `healthPercent`, `transition`, `away` |
| src/gear_snapshot.cpp | the guest's gained gear as a piece list (slot, kind, category, durability): multiset `minus`, text `format` / `parse` (unit tested) | `gear_snapshot::minus`, `format`, `parse` |
| src/gear_restore.cpp | adapter.ini `gear_restore=1` (off by default): while a guest, writes what Sam gained since the join (backpack and worn pieces) to `coop/personal_gear.txt` every 30 s on change; at the next settled join gives the snapshot back through `game::addCargo` / `addSlotPiece` minus what Sam already holds | `gear_restore::installEarly`, `tick`, `setEnabled` |
| src/ds2/local_status.cpp | the local player's health (entity GetHealth through enemy_vitals), dead flag (an unreadable entity is unknown, not dead), loading screen, driving and menu (world_pause::menuUp or the cargo menu), for the status word | `game::localStatus` |
| src/config.cpp | `coop/adapter.ini` (port 27980, overlay, self_marker, remote_body = the partner's body, on by default; enemy_sync = shared enemies and weapon_sync = weapon sync, both on by default (0 turns them off); weapon_attach_mode) | `loadConfig` |
| src/documents_redirect.cpp | session saves: with `coop\session\Documents` present, the game's import slots for SHGetKnownFolderPath / SHGetFolderPathW return that folder for Documents (patched from DllMain) | `documents_redirect::install`, `active` |
| src/import_patch.cpp | points one of the game executable's own import slots at a detour (save redirect, raw input filter) | `import_patch::redirect` |
| src/input_filter.cpp | redirects the game's GetRawInputData import: key presses an adapter menu claims reach the game as an unknown key, releases always pass | `input_filter::install` |
| src/game.h | the engine interface the rest of the adapter uses: resolve, local player pose, render camera, a per-frame simulation function, the guest restriction, bodies, vehicles, the backpack's cargo and loose cargo | `game::resolve`, `watchInteractions`, `blockScriptedInteractions`, `drivenVehicle`, `placeVehicle`, `vehicleCargo`, `addVehicleCargo`, `localPlayer`, `camera`, `frameFunction`, `borrowBody`, `placeBody`, `carriedCargo`, `looseCargo`, `addCargo`, `placeCargo`, `removeCargo` |
| src/ds2/game.cpp | DS2 implementation: byte patterns for Player::GetLocalPlayer (PlayerManager global) and Player::GetLastActivatedCamera (camera stack offsets); Player -> Entity -> WorldTransform; CameraEntity FOV / near plane | `findCode`, `localPlayerObject`, `lastActivatedCamera` |
| src/decima/world_transform.h | Decima WorldPosition (doubles) / RotMatrix / WorldTransform layouts with static_asserts | `decima::WorldTransform` |
| src/decima/safe_read.cpp | SEH-guarded game memory reads | `decima::safeRead`, `readPointer` |
| src/pattern_scan.cpp | byte-pattern search in the game's .text, rip-relative targets | `pattern_scan::find`, `ripTarget` |
| src/player_sync.cpp | a guest that hears the host announced again (a new join serial after the first, so the host was unreachable and came back inside the launcher's grace) asks `resync::kAll` from the host; the net thread's loop: PLAYER_STATE (0x0100, 24 bytes: seq, pos, yaw, status word) at 60 Hz; newest state per peer, ordered by seq, velocity between reports (passed on to the body), extrapolated position; join/leave/death toasts; tells the game layer whether this machine is a guest (use-location gate); hands frames and ticks to cargo_transfer and cargo_ground | `player_sync::start`, `remotePlayers`, `PlayerState` |
| src/world_to_screen.h | pure pinhole projection from a horizontal field of view (unit tested), plus the off-screen edge arrow | `world_to_screen::project`, `edgeArrow` (screen-edge position and direction of an off-screen point) |
| src/dx12_hook.cpp | hooks CreateSwapChain(ForHwnd) (remembers the game's queue), ExecuteCommandLists (fallback queue), Present / Present1; Dear ImGui DX12 renderer on the game's back buffers, rebuilt when the game recreates its swap chain; a fault disables drawing | `dx12_hook::install` |
| src/ds2/frame_gen.h/.cpp | the game's frame generation setting (registry HKCU ...\Graphics FrameGen: 0 off, 1 DLSS, 2 FSR, 3 XeSS); dx12_hook reads it at every swap chain creation and draws nothing under FSR or XeSS, with one log line per change (unit tested) | `frame_gen::current`, `fromValue`, `overlaySafe` |
| tests/frame_gen_test.cpp | value mapping and which modes keep the overlay | |
| src/remote_body.h | the partner's body API: `setEnabled`, `installEarly` (engine hooks, on unless adapter.ini says `remote_body=0`), `setTarget` (simulation thread, from peer_pose; carries whether the partner is dead or loading, which parks the body),  implemented in src/ds2/remote_player.cpp | `remote_body::setTarget`, `installEarly` |
| src/ds2/remote_player.cpp | the partner's body, a second player entity: created like a net player (NetPlayerGame, shared references, entity resource, AddPlayer, RequestSpawn) once Sam's state machine has run 8 s and a peer reports (forgotten again when it stops: return to title, reload); own camera and silenced components right after the spawn; each frame (hook on the object-list update 0x140215460) it rides the vehicle the peer drives or is placed at the peer's pose | `remote_body::*`, `remote_player::entity`, `player`, `slot`, `isLive`; a body whose peer sent no pose for 5 s is parked (hidden with `ds2::setEntityVisible`, not placed) and shown again when a peer reports, never removed mid-world; the body is taken down  when the world is left: gameplay ended, `remote_player::leave` / `requestLeave` (RequestChangeArea hook); spawn and sim-tick gates share `sim_tick::gameplayActive` / `gameplaySettled`; no body is built while the loading screen is up or for 3 s after it |
| src/ds2/remote_camera.cpp | the remote's own DSThirdPersonPlayerCameraComponent and DSCameraMode (index patched to -1 for the mode init); skips the component's Init / Update / Teleported and the mode builder for the remote | `remote_camera::give`, `SpawnScope`, `installEarly` |
| src/ds2/remote_baggage.cpp | detour on the baggage carrier registration 0x14123bad0: for  after the remote spawns, its carriers get their own owner/parent hashes (component +0x68 / +0x6C) so registering the remote's owners does not adopt Sam's backpack | `remote_baggage::installEarly`, `beginSpawn`; the carriers registered while this thread is in remote_camera's SpawnScope are the remote's |
| src/ds2/remote_marker.cpp | the marker manager (global 0x14623E638) keeps groups of one marker (player marker type 1, backpack marker type 25); `snapshot` before the remote spawns and `repair` for 20 s after take the remote's markers out of the array and point the groups back at Sam's, so the unload no longer leaves a freed marker for the HUD loop | `remote_marker::snapshot`, `repair` |
| src/ds2/remote_guards.cpp | player id table sized 4, art part toggle bound check for the remote, the components that write the shared HUD block / act on Sam silenced (handler list entries cleared; also the equipment-manage and facial-rig removal handlers); their classes are looked up at start-up, so a spawn does not scan the image | `remote_guards::installEarly`, `silenceRemote` |
| src/ds2/remote_update.cpp/.h | what the remote body's DSPlayerComponent update runs (it starts the rides but also flickers Sam's prompts): the update and nine of the calls it makes are hooked, and the remote runs the whole update except the motion call's ability block (it flickers Sam's use prompt; the call's tail, the state machine step that starts a ride, still runs); a skip mask for the remote only (`playerupdate.txt`, default 0) is kept for bisecting; every change of the remote's state object / vtable is logged | `remote_update::installEarly`, `setSkipMask` |
| src/ds2/player_system_guard.cpp/.h | DSPlayerSystem's singleton pointers: every load of the system pointer inside the equipment-manage and facial-rig inits (0x140F69970, 0x140E86A80) reads a cell, set per call to Sam's system or, for the remote's components, a scratch block; the equipment component's per-frame update (0x140F6B9B0) is skipped for the remote (`setEquipmentUpdateSkipped`, test command `equpdate.txt` turns it back on) | `player_system_guard::installEarly`, `setEquipmentUpdateSkipped` |
| src/ds2/remote_appearance.cpp | ticks the remote's body-variant loader after Sam's, asking for Sam's variant once (the costume) | `remote_appearance::installEarly`, `onSpawned` |
| src/ds2/remote_animation.cpp | the remote copies another player's animation: before the graph evaluates it (MsgGetAnimatedPose hooks) every graph-bound animation variable of the source (the partner's reports from anim_sync, or the local player with adapter.ini mirror_animation=1) is written; also samples the local player's changes for anim_sync into the remote's AnimationManager with the engine's setters; after the partner's state it writes the partner's active pulses (anim_event) | `remote_animation::installEarly`, `setMirrorLocalPlayer` |
| src/ds2/remote_ride.cpp | (door and passenger pod offsets per vehicle model, keyed by the entity resource UUID in `kSeats`; an unlisted model is logged and not boarded; the local passenger gets off through the game's ride-off when the driver leaves) boards the vehicle the peer drives (vehicle_sync::drivenVehicleId) through the game's own ride request ([owner+0x3970] = 3, target id at [owner+0x39e0], remote at the driver door) and leaves when the peer stops (requested phase 3, ClearParent when RideOff ends); when the local player drives the same vehicle, or the peer reports a passenger seat, it rides along in the second seat pod; the local player as passenger of the partner's vehicle: F poll writes the ride request, his mover update seats him in the second pod, `g_samSeated` (plugin reached Drive) makes `endLocalRide` clear his parent link exactly once when the plugin is back on foot, however the ride ended | `remote_ride::tick`, `holdsBody`, `seatLocalPassenger`, `endLocalRide` |
| src/ds2/ride_plugin.h | field offsets of the DSPlayerRideVehicleActionPlugin (phase, requested phase, target, owner) and of the owner's ride request, shared by remote_ride and ride_probe | `ride_plugin::kPhase`, `kOwnerRequestKind` |
| src/ds2/ride_probe.cpp | probe P1, log-only, on only with adapter.ini `diagnostics=1`: for the local player's ride plugin, each change in what TryStart 0x1410047d0 and its prompt gate 0x141011b20 decide (request kind, prompt gate, global block, reach, blockers, targets), so a live F press on a partner-driven vehicle shows where the confirm stops | `ride_probe::installEarly` |
| src/ds2/remote_context.cpp | while the entity dispatcher runs for the remote, the helpers that answer "the player with local index 0" (controller, param query, controlled entity, notify, send) answer for the remote | `remote_context::enter`, `leave`, `active`, `install` |
| src/ds2/setdriver_guard.cpp | Vehicle::SetDriver for the remote keeps the vehicle manager's local driven vehicle and last-driven ids as they were; when the vehicle already has a driver the remote's enter / leave are answered as done (passenger); the local player's own enter / leave on a vehicle the partner drives are answered the same way and recorded (`localPassengerVehicle`, which vehicle_sync reports as the passenger seat), and the free gate is opened for the vehicle the partner drives | `setdriver_guard::install`, `localPassengerVehicle`, `endLocalPassenger` |
| src/ds2/player_state.cpp | a player entity's DSPlayerState: its ride plugin, whether its state machine runs, and `GameplayClock` (how long it has run: gameplay counts as started after 8 s, since the plugin turns active during the load after Continue) | `ds2::ridePlugin`, `inGameplay`, `GameplayClock` |

| src/ds2/enemy_puppet.cpp | guest: tamed enemies by UUID; the host's announcement binds one, its states place it every tick, its death kills it, its disappearance removes it; every puppet is checked against the entity map first; `animate` (asked by every animated entity's pose evaluation) writes the host's animation variables of a puppet near the partner and returns at once while no puppet has any | `enemy_puppet::adopt`, `adoptExisting`, `releaseToHost` (this machine became host: tamed enemies get their AI back and go to enemy_host), `animate`, `game::puppetSpawn`, `puppetStates`, `puppetAnim`, `puppetGone` |
| src/ds2/entity_lookup.cpp, src/ds2/enemy_pose.h | whether the engine still holds an entity by UUID (EntityManagerGame's map); an entity transform as enemy_wire::Pose | `ds2::entityExists`, `enemy_pose::toWire`, `fromWire` |
| src/enemy_sync.cpp, src/enemy_wire.h | ENEMY_SPAWN / ENEMY_STATE / ENEMY_GONE / ENEMY_ANIM (0x011B-0x011E; ENEMY_ANIM: the animation variables of enemies within 50 m of the partner's body): the host sends, the guest applies; join snapshot via RESYNC kEnemies | `enemy_sync::onFrame`, `tick`, `enemy_wire::decodeOne`, `encodeStates`, `decodeStates`, `encodeAnim`, `decodeAnim` |
| src/struct_wire.h | STRUCT_REQUEST (0x012A, guest to host, same payload with id kAssignId) and STRUCT_CREATE (0x010F: kind, sub-kind, level, id, guid, transform, durability, the kind's tail) and STRUCT_REMOVE (0x0110: id, factor) payloads; ladders only (`supported`) | `struct_wire::Create`, `Remove`, `Placed`, `encode`, `decode` |
| src/peer_joins.h | `PeerJoins`: which peer joins a host has answered, keyed by slot and the net client's PEER_UP serial (`PeerInfo::joinSerial`), so a peer that takes the slot of one the host has not dropped yet is still a join (used by struct_sync, fact_sync, enemy_sync, story_sync, weapon_sync instead of "the peer count rose") | `PeerJoins::takeNew`, `clear` |
| src/struct_sync.cpp | host: sends the placed and removed structures each tick and builds a guest's STRUCT_REQUEST; guest: sends its own refused placements and collapses to the host (the host removes and announces); guest: decodes them and queues them for the game layer | `struct_sync::onFrame`, `tick` |
| src/weapon_wire.h | WEAPON_STATE (0x0124, reliable, on change: drawn weapon id and hand, id 0 = holstered) and WEAPON_FIRE (0x0125, reliable, one per shot or throw: weapon id, behavior kind, pellet count, origin, direction); `encode`, `decode` with range checks. 0x0126-0x0127 are reserved for it, unused | `weapon_wire::WeaponState`, `WeaponFire`, `decode` |
| src/weapon_sync.cpp | net thread of weapon sync (adapter.ini `weapon_sync`, on by default): polls the local drawn weapon every 100 ms and reports a change (and to a peer that just joined), sends the shots the hooks recorded, keeps the newest state per peer and tells the game layer the one of the peer the body stands for; shots from other peers are dropped | `weapon_sync::onFrame`, `tick`, `setEnabled` |
| src/ds2/weapon_layout.cpp/.h | the engine's weapon layout in named constants (weapon table of the player entity, table entry, weapon entity, behavior component, the seven CreateAttackRequest functions, vtable slot 46); a weapon's id and owner; the behavior component that makes shots (found by its slot 46) | `ds2::weapon::weaponId`, `weaponOwner`, `shotBehavior`, `kShotFunctions` |
| src/ds2/local_weapon.cpp | the local side: `game::localWeaponState` reads the current entry of Sam's weapon table (drawn flag, weapon id; logs the table index, the entry's flag bytes and the weapon entity's +0x98 whenever one changes), detours on the seven CreateAttackRequest functions record each shot whose weapon is owned by Sam (enemies run the same functions) | `local_weapon::installEarly`, `game::localWeaponState`, `takeLocalFires` |
| src/ds2/remote_weapon.cpp | the remote side: gives the partner's body its drawn weapon through the engine's own table creation 0x140eab2f0 on a free entry of the body's real weapon table, then sets the table's current index to it (owner, attach, components as for Sam), removes it through the entry reset 0x140eab0f0 and index 0 when the weapon changes, sets the table's current and requested indices (+0x18C0, +0x18C4), gives the weapon's bullet damage attack type (`remote_weapon::attackType`) and, for each shot, points the behavior's aim target (+0x390) along the partner's shot and sets its fire request byte +0x4D1, logging the weapon's position, parent and +0x98 flags when made and after 1 s; never touches Sam's table | `remote_weapon::installEarly`, `game::setPartnerWeapon`, `partnerFire` |
| src/ds2/damage_veto.cpp | detour on the damage queue 0x140129de0: damage whose instigator is the partner's body (or its child, the weapon) is not queued unless the combat sync applies it (`remote_apply`) | `damage_veto::installEarly` |
| src/story_wire.h | STORY_EVENT (0x0119, host to all, reliable; a guest sends OrderRequest and OrderDelivered to the host, which completes the mission): kind (mission start/success/fail, section active/inactive, area change, order request), mission id, args, section UUID, area transform; `decode` | `story_wire::Event`, `decode` |
| src/story_replay.h | the guest's rule for a host mission event: a start replays only for a mission not started here, success/fail only for one in progress (tested in story_wire_test) | `applies` |
| src/story_ledger.h | host ledger of started missions with their real start arguments and section; a joining or resyncing guest gets those starts replayed (no-row fallback for a mission from a save); cleared at each load (tested in story_wire_test) | `Ledger::startFor` |
| src/story_sync.cpp | host: sends the story events each tick and starts a mission snapshot for a joined or resyncing peer; guest: decodes events, queues them for replay, forwards its order requests to the host (the host runs them), asks for the snapshot at gameplay | `story_sync::onFrame`, `tick` |
| src/order_handoff.cpp | host: a guest's accepted order (story.cpp replays its OrderRequest and calls `expect`) is watched by a sim-tick scan (500 ms, `Gate::Gameplay`) until the mission is in progress and the same non-empty set of its pieces (`game::orderPieces`, outside every backpack) is seen twice; the net thread then gives each piece to the guest with `cargo_transfer::handOver`; dropped after 30 s | `order_handoff::installEarly`, `expect`, `tick` |
| src/ds2/orders_diag.cpp | log-only instruments for the partner-cargo checks, on only with adapter.ini `orders_diagnostics=1`: the callers of the carrier headline getter 0x1414a13a0, the callers and answer of the owner-active check 0x14119b2e0 for the remote's owner, the owner the carrier panel 0x141569dc0 shows, whether the hand-over gather had to append the remote's owner, and who calls the carried-by-player set and how much the scoped walk added | `orders_diag::installEarly`, `noteHandOverGather`, `noteCarriedSet` |
| src/ds2/partner_cargo.cpp | the carried-by-player set 0x1411d7530 also walks the remote's owner (delivery and order checks count the partner's rack); the hand-over gather 0x14119b930 gets the remote's owner appended when it carries order pieces; the baggage slot add/remove hooks report a piece that moves between the remote's owner and a terminal or any locker or the local player's owner (left), or that any other known owner puts into the remote's (given); on a guest the same hooks, with the remove recorded, report a piece the local player puts into or takes out of a shared locker (`lockers::noteLocalMove`); `diagnostics=1` logs every slot call that touches a locker, kind 27 to 29, with its caller (probe P-L2) | `partner_cargo::installEarly(diagnostics)`, `game::takePartnerMoves`, `baggageOwner` |
| src/partner_cargo_sync.cpp, src/partner_cargo_wire.h | CARGO_GONE (0x011A, to the partner, either direction): what the partner's rack lost to a terminal or to this player, the partner deletes its copy; the host's gives into the partner's rack go as CARGO_ADD with the order link, a guest's as CARGO_GIVE (0x0133); both are held for rack_sync until the partner's MOVE_ACK (0x0130, sent once its own CARGO_LIST shows the move) or the link drops | `partner_cargo_sync::onFrame`, `tick`, `expectAdded`, `acknowledge`, `recentlyLeft`, `recentlyGiven` |
| src/ds2/story.cpp | the host polls the mission map for state changes (sim tick, 500 ms; the baseline restarts at each gameplay epoch, so a load is not reported) and detours the section request and RequestChangeArea; a guest vetoes the request functions and replays the host's events through them (bit 18 preset on starts) | `story::installEarly`, `game::setStoryRole`, `requestStorySnapshot`, `takeStoryRequests`, `forwardOrderRequest`, `missionState`, `takeStoryEvents`, `replayStoryEvent`; the host's replay of a guest's OrderRequest tells `order_handoff` |
| src/ds2/structures.cpp | hooks the structure submit 0x141263280 (player placements by caller 0x14200cc0d) and RequestRemove of a ladder; guest builds with factory + Init + submit and removes through RemoveConstructionByPlayer 0x1412d9c80 on the simulation tick; refuses the guest's own placements | `structures::installEarly`, `game::setStructureRole`, `takePlacedStructures`, `takeRemovedStructures`, `buildStructure`, `removeStructure` |
| src/ds2/sim_tick.cpp | the one detour on the engine's per-frame object update 0x140215460 (`frame()` is its counter); modules register a function that runs ahead of it on the simulation thread (remote body, structures), optionally gated on the local player's state machine running (camps, structures, story, world clock); logs frames/s and the callbacks' cost every 5 s | `sim_tick::installEarly`, `add`, `Gate`, `gameplayEpoch`; the one world signal: `inWorld` (state machine running, no loading screen), `worldBuilt` (loading screen gone 1 s), `worldReady` (settled 8 s and the screen gone 3 s), `gameplayActive` (state machine alone: stops only when the world is torn down), `gameplaySettled` (state machine running 8 s; also `game::gameplaySettled`, the gate of the fact queue and of the guests' join snapshot requests) |
| src/time_us.h | `TimeUs` and `nowUs()`: the monotonic microsecond clock every network timestamp uses | `nowUs` |
| src/authority_wire.h | AUTH_CLAIM / AUTH_DECLINE / AUTH_STOP (0x0111-0x0113, reliable, to all) payload `AuthMessage {id, epoch, owner}` and its decode | `authority_wire::AuthMessage`, `decode` |
| src/authority.cpp | the authority table `{object id, owner slot, epoch}` (pure logic): claim, assign (host), release, stop (host), decline; stale epochs dropped, barred-owner list (no owner bounce), two claims of one epoch settled by the lower slot, a leaving slot loses its objects, a change handler | `authority::AuthorityTable`, `onMessage`, `pickOwner`, `claimsOf`, `dropSlot` |
| src/authority_sync.cpp | the one table of the adapter on the net thread: local actions queued and sent reliably to all, peers' messages applied (rejects counted), a leaving peer's objects released, own claims repeated to a joining peer or on RESYNC; the API vehicles and enemies will use | `authority_sync::claim`, `release`, `decline`, `isOwner`, `ownerOf`, `onFrame`, `tick` |
| src/clock_offset.h | peer clock offset from ping/pong samples (pure): median of the last 16 offsets, valid from 3 samples, peer time to local time | `ClockOffset::addSample`, `toLocal` |
| src/clock_sync.cpp | CLOCK_PING / CLOCK_PONG (0x0114 / 0x0115, unreliable, to one slot): pings every peer (250 ms until 16 samples, then 2 s), answers pings at once, keeps one `ClockOffset` per peer | `clock_sync::toLocalUs`, `rttUs`, `onFrame`, `tick` |
| src/adaptive_delay.h | how far behind real time a stream is rendered (pure): 150-300 ms, grows at once on a late report, shrinks 10 ms per 3 s, lateness measured against the fastest recent transit | `AdaptiveDelay::onArrival`, `delayUs` |
| src/anim_change.h | one animation variable (`Change`: index, type, value) and `valueBytes`, shared by the codec and `ds2/remote_animation` | `remote_animation::Change` |

| src/pulse_detector.h | finds one-shot edges (a boolean that leaves its resting value and returns within 0.5 s) in the reported variables (pure) | `PulseDetector::onBool` |
| src/anim_event.cpp | ANIM_EVENT receiver: holds each received pulse 100 ms; `ds2/remote_animation` writes the active ones on top of the partner's state | `anim_event::onFrame`, `active` |

| src/reject_counters.cpp | per message type and reason (stale epoch, unknown object, wrong sender, invalid owner, malformed, expired, overflow) counts, one `rejects:` log line every 5 s while they change | `reject_counters::count`, `total`, `summaryIfDue` |
| src/resync.cpp | RESYNC (0x0118, reliable): "send me your full state" with scopes facts / authority / anim; modules ask `takeRequests(scope)` from their tick; `request` is inline in resync.h | `resync::request`, `requestLocal`, `onFrame`, `takeRequests` |

| src/ds2/health_watch.cpp | in-process hardware write watch (Dr0 on every thread through a helper thread, vectored handler recording the writing instruction and stack returns, logged on the sim tick); armed by test command `watchhealth.txt "net id"` on an enemy's life float (`enemy_vitals::healthAddress`) or `watch.txt "address"`; installed only with test_commands=1 | `health_watch::arm`, `disarm`, `installEarly` |
| src/ds2/combat_hook.cpp | the hook on ApplyDamage 0x1406fd570 (guest: puppet hits diverted; host: hits on the remote body diverted), building received hits the way script damage does (attack link, borrowed context, DamageParams) under `remote_apply::Scope` ; adapter.ini `god_mode=1` (honoured only with test_commands=1) drops every hit on the local player (`setGodMode`) | `setGodMode`, `combat_hook::installEarly`, `game::setCombatRole`, `takeEnemyHits`, `takePlayerHits`, `applyEnemyHit`, `applyPlayerHit`, `enemyAlive` |

## adapters/sr2/ (C++20, x86, dinput8.dll proxy, Saints Row 2)

Build and tests: `adapters/sr2/rebuild.cmd`, `adapters/sr2/test.cmd` (vcvars32, cmake + ninja, output `adapters/sr2/build/dinput8.dll`, copied to the git-ignored `adapters/sr2/dinput8.dll` that the launcher profile and the release package use). Compiles the logger, paths, MinHook wrapper and MinHook from `adapters/re0` (the log file name comes from the `COOP_LOG_FILE` define: `<game>\coop\sr2_adapter.log`). Notes and addresses: `docs/SR2_NOTES.md`.

| file | owns | key members |
|---|---|---|
| src/proxy.cpp | `DirectInput8Create` forwarded to the system DLL; DllMain pins the module and starts the init thread | `DirectInput8Create`, `DllMain` |
| src/init.cpp | start-up order: log, ini, build check (exe and replication sites), partner, activity and players 3 and 4 site checks (a difference only drops that logic), the tunnel (milestone 10: table check and write, or the native fallback) and the game socket option, the script scan and the content hash (milestone 11 B3, always), version gate, hooks, player cap edits, game port (E1..E3 only on the native transport), the own address rule (native only), force join (milestone 6), the three site groups (the last, `multiplayer_patches`, needs the player cap's leave edits for its guest leave phase), then the anim and asset streaming phases of milestone 16 W1 (`anim_stream`, with `crowd`; | src/net_clock.cpp, src/net_clock_rules.h, src/player_sites.h | milestone 11 B1, fast fire on player 3's screen (other players' and NPCs' guns fired faster than their refire): every replicated object plays its input history back against the session net clock T (`[session+8]`), which the `clock` handler set from any sender, backward too, replaying recent history; on a guest with 2 or more remote members CK1 (hook 0x7FCBF0, cdecl(payload, context)) takes only the host's clock (`[session+0x404]`) and a clock behind T becomes a debt instead of a step back, CK2 (hook 0x7FC930, the net tick) pays the debt with at most half of the time the tick added (measured as T before and after the game's tick); a debt above 1000 ms is a resync as the game does; the host and 2-player play run the game's code; `trace=1` logs every clock message (`net clock: from slot ..`); pure rules (`governs`, `onClock`, `tickAdvance`, `onTick`), site tables `kNetClock` (CK1, CK2), `kBundleSender` (BUT), and, milestone 14, `kCutsceneVisuals` (PV1, PV2 and the checks of their resume points), `kSkinPool` (PV3 to PV10) and `kAnimTrace` (AN1 to AN3) | `net_clock::bind`, `setTrace`, `net_clock_rules::governs`, `onClock`, `onTick`, `tickAdvance`, `kMaxClockDebtMs` |

## release/ (repo root)

| file | owns |
|---|---|
| .github/workflows/release.yml | on push to main: build the RE0 and SR2 adapters (x86, with their unit tests), publish launcher (self-contained), assemble the package (`launcher\app`, `launcher\games\re0.json`, `adapters\re0\dinput8.dll`, `adapters\sr2\dinput8.dll`, `README.txt`, `version.txt` = run number, `update.json` = this repo), zip `RE0-Coop.zip`, replace the release tagged `latest` (title `Build <run number>`) |
| launcher/package/README.txt | player README shipped in the package |
| launcher/update.json | `{ "repo": "Precyse/RE0Together" }` copied next to the exe; a missing file or the `OWNER/REPO` placeholder disables updating (the workflow writes the real repo into the package) |

## Where to look for

- The web window (WebView2 host, bridge, snapshots, commands, images, security settings): `launcher/Web/` (table above); the page: `launcher/web/` with `launcher/web/SPEC.md`; a screen's data: `Web/UiSnapshot*.cs`; what a page command does: `Web/UiController.Actions.cs`; the classic WinForms window is still built with `-p:LauncherUi=Classic`; the tests are `tools/launcher_tests/UiCommandsTests.cs`, `UiSnapshotTests.cs`, `UiContractTests.cs` (only these: `LAUNCHER_TESTS_ONLY=web`)
- GUI: `launcher/Gui/MainForm.cs` composes the views in `launcher/Gui/` (what the window says for a status: `Gui/SessionCards.cs`; the screens: `Gui/Screens.cs`, `Gui/ScreenHost.cs`, `Gui/MainForm.Screens.cs`; the home: `Gui/HomeScreen.cs`, `Gui/HeroBanner.cs`; the lobby browser and popups: `Gui/LobbiesScreen.cs`, `Gui/LobbyRowModels.cs`, `Gui/LobbyPopup.cs`, `Gui/OptionsPopup.cs`; the update button: `Gui/UpdateFlow.cs`); settings (file `%AppData%\CoopLauncher\settings.json`): `AppSettings.cs` and `Gui/SettingsView.cs`; session logic stays in `App.cs`; preview builds of `launcher-*` branches: `.github/workflows/launcher-preview.yml` (prerelease `launcher-preview`)
- Motion (durations, curves, recipes ported from the broadcast tool): `launcher/Gui/Motion.cs`
- RE0 outfit art / character select: `launcher/Gui/OutfitArt.cs`, `launcher/MtFramework.cs`, `launcher/Gui/CharacterSelectView.cs` (what it shows: `launcher/Gui/SelectModel.cs`; the profile data: `outfitArt`, `character` and `art` in `launcher/games/re0.json`)
- Auto-update or release packaging: `Updater.cs`, `.github/workflows/release.yml`
- Wire format or control messages: `Framing.cs`, `ControlMessages.cs`
- Slot / epoch rules: `Session.cs`, `SlotAssigner.cs`
- Session start, refusals, held-session rejoin, host left / re-host, the status and step the loop publishes, the options written to the adapter ini on launch: `App.cs` (`StartSession`, `Refuse`, `AdapterOptions`), `App.Reconnect.cs`, `App.Status.cs`; the start checks: `StartChecks.cs`; a rendezvous game's join: `NativeJoin.cs`
- Adapter link behaviour: `LoopbackBridge.cs`
- Add a game: new `launcher/games/<id>.json` (DS2: `ds2.json`, adapter `adapters/ds2`, notes `docs/DS2_NOTES.md`, stages `docs/DS2_ROADMAP.md`; SR2: `sr2.json`, a native session, adapter `adapters/sr2`, notes `docs/SR2_NOTES.md`)
- Native `steam_api64.dll` (Valve-signed redistributable from the Steamworks.NET 2024.8.0 standalone release, SDK 1.60): `launcher/native/`
- Items (inventory blocks): `inventory_sync.cpp`; floor items: `floor_items_sync.cpp`; layouts in `docs/RE0_NOTES.md` Items
- RE0 floor-item takes in state mode (who may take a herb, why `pickup_guard: take refused` appeared, the host's grant log): `src/item_ledger.cpp` and `src/item_ledger_rule.h`; the held engine step is `src/pickup_guard.cpp`; notes `docs/RE0_NOTES.md` "State mode: item ledger"
- What RE0 co-op still lacks (done / not needed / open): `docs/re/RE0_COOP_GAPS.md`
- RE0 game addresses: `adapters/re0/src/game.h` (source: `docs/RE0_NOTES.md`)
- RE0 room script events (cutscenes, scripted spawns, examine events): `event_sync.cpp` and `event_rule.h`, design `docs/re/RE0_EVENT_SYNC.md`, read a room's script with `tools/re0/bes2.py`, its trigger zones with `tools/re0/evc2.py`
- Save sync: `SaveSyncCoordinator.cs` (launcher), `save_redirect.cpp` (adapter)
- Which launcher process may be closed, one window per session: `SingleInstance.cs`, `InstanceGuard.cs` (never start the window from a test: it closes other launchers)
- Data folder of tests and the UI harness: environment variable `COOP_LAUNCHER_DATA` (`AppData.cs`)
- Mod install, backups, repair, uninstall, folder entries and their manifest: `ModInstaller.cs`, `ModFiles.cs`; update staging and rollback: `Updater.cs`; report zip: `ReportBundle.cs`
- Guest logs on the host: `LogForwarder.cs`, written to `<game>\coop\peer_<steamid>_<stamp>.log` (newest 10 per peer)
- Adapter network behaviour: `adapters/re0/src/net_client.cpp`
- Control rule (who drives a character): `control_rule.h` (pure) applied in `character_owner::controlOf`; party commands: `command_input.cpp`, `party_mode.cpp`, `camera_parity.cpp` (trace a press or decision in `adapter.log`, lines starting `command:`); pickup crash guard: `pickup_guard.cpp`
- RE0 state mode switches (`partner_mode=state`): is it on `state_mode.cpp`, sending a state-block message `state_net.cpp`, receiving one with its sender policy `frame_router.cpp`, the room load counter `load_serial.h` / `scene::loadSerial`; notes `docs/RE0_NOTES.md` "State mode: plumbing"
- Who runs a room's enemies in state mode (`partner_mode=state`): `room_authority.cpp` (`runsHere`), rules `room_authority_rule.h`, wire `room_authority_wire.h`; read by `door_travel::enemyAuthority` and `split_rooms::localEnemyAuthority`; notes `docs/RE0_NOTES.md` "State mode: room authority"
- RE0 slow frames and which module took the time (state mode): `slow_frame_rule.h`, `slow_frame.cpp`, timing scopes in `game_tick.cpp`; the launcher side of the newest-wins lane `launcher/FreshQueue.cs` + `Session.cs`; notes `docs/RE0_NOTES.md` "State mode: transport and diagnostics"
- RE0 enemies shown from the other machine's stream in state mode (why an enemy froze, holds, or did not follow; the CharPause path): receiver, hook and per-slot state `enemy_observer.cpp`, simulator side `enemy_observer_capture.cpp`, engine reads and writes `enemy_observer_game.cpp`, rules `enemy_observer_rule.h`, `enemy_observer_send_rule.h`, wire `enemy_observer_wire.h`, class policy `enemy_policy.h`; trace lines start `enemy_observer:`; notes `docs/RE0_NOTES.md` "State mode: enemy observers"
- RE0 enemies shown from the other machine's stream in state mode (why an enemy froze, holds, or did not follow; the CharPause path): receiver, hook and per-slot state `enemy_observer.cpp`, simulator side `enemy_observer_capture.cpp`, engine reads and writes `enemy_observer_game.cpp`, rules `enemy_observer_rule.h`, `enemy_observer_send_rule.h`, wire `enemy_observer_wire.h`, class policy `enemy_policy.h`; trace lines start `enemy_observer:`; notes `docs/RE0_NOTES.md` "State mode: enemy observers"; who takes a hit on an observed enemy, hand-over when the authority changes, creation of a missing enemy and the door barrier being off in state mode: `enemy_net.cpp` (`routeOwnHit`, forwarding), `enemy_observer.cpp` (`handOver`, `simulatorOf`), `enemy_spawn.cpp` (`createOnDemand`), `room_gate.cpp`; notes `docs/RE0_NOTES.md` "State mode: enemy hits and hand-over"
- RE0 enemy attacks on the remote character in state mode (why the other player took no damage, a plain hit, a grab that moved the enemy to the victim's machine, a stuck or expired lease): router `player_contact.cpp`, plain hits `contact_net.cpp`, lease `enemy_lease.cpp`, rules `contact_rule.h` and `enemy_lease_rule.h`, wire `enemy_lease_wire.h`, engine reads `contact_game.cpp`; trace lines start `contact:` and `lease:`; notes `docs/RE0_NOTES.md` "State mode: contact and lease"
- RE0 boss fights when the room's authority changes (`partner_mode=state`: a boss that stopped, a `boss:` log line, a hand-over that froze the members): module `boss_handover.cpp`, rules and stages `boss_handover_rule.h`, wire `boss_wire.h`, the freeze / snapshot / resume / startAt calls it makes `enemy_observer.h`; notes `docs/RE0_NOTES.md` "State mode: boss hand-over"
- RE0 join, rejoin and resync in state mode (`partner_mode=state`: the guest never becomes caught up, `session_ready:` or `join_extras:` log lines, the host's extras): `session_ready.cpp` (handshake) and `session_ready_rule.h` (rules), `join_extras.cpp` (what the host tells beyond the snapshot), `session_wire.h` (wire), `join_sync.cpp` (`placed`, `caughtUp`); a save that does not go through while the partner moves, `save_gate.cpp` and `save_gate_rule.h`; a death that does not end the game on the survivor, `player_damage.cpp` `requestGameOver`; notes `docs/RE0_NOTES.md` "State mode: session"
- RE0 requests, proposals and floor events from before a save load (state mode: why the host dropped a guest message, `world_epoch: dropped`, a take refused `stale`, a flag change that appeared after Continue, floor items that duplicated or vanished after a reload): `src/world_epoch.cpp` (+ `world_epoch_rule.h`), the hold across a load `src/flag_load_hold_rule.h` + `flag_sync::onLocalLoad`, the load hook `session_slot.cpp` `onLoad`, the floor queues `floor_items_sync::onLoad`; open review findings F4 to F14: `docs/RE0_NOTES.md` "State mode: sync review fixes"
- RE0 host-ordered story flags in state mode (who orders a flag change, why a `flag_seq: corrected` line appeared, where a serial gap asks for a snapshot): `src/flag_sequencer.cpp` and `src/flag_sequencer_rule.h`; applying a flag (setter, enemy bits) is still `src/flag_sync.cpp`
- RE0 weapon effects of the remote character in state mode (what a shot, reload, throw or dry fire shows on the puppet, why `fx unlisted` counts, adding a missing effect site): sites `weapon_fx_sites.h`, wire `weapon_fx_wire.h`, owner `weapon_fx_capture.cpp`, receiver `weapon_fx_replay.cpp`, engine calls `weapon_fx_game.cpp`; the projectile damage attribution is the state-mode branch in `enemy_damage_hook.cpp`; notes `docs/RE0_NOTES.md` "State mode: weapon effects"
- RE0 push blocks in state mode (why a box did not move on the other screen, who fired its zone trigger, a lease that stayed held or was denied): lease and commit rules `prop_rule.h`, wire `prop_wire.h`, net and hook `prop_sync.cpp`, engine access `prop_game.cpp`, trigger class `event_rule.h` (`TriggerKind::Obstacle`) used by `event_sync.cpp` `startDetour`; notes `docs/RE0_NOTES.md` "State mode: props"
- RE0 script body ops on the owner and the shared cutscene skip (`partner_mode=state`): rules `event_rule.h` (`OpRole::Body`, `runFor`), decision `event_body.cpp` called from `event_sync.cpp` `runAsSubject`, skip `demo_skip.cpp` with `demo_skip_rule.h` / `demo_skip_wire.h`, results `docs/RE0_NOTES.md` "State mode: scripts and cutscenes"
- RE0 pose puppet (`partner_mode=state`: the remote-owned character shown from the owner's pose instead of its replayed pad): wire `pose_wire.h`, timeline `pose_timeline.h` on the shared `stream_clock.h` and `timed_queue.h`, rules `puppet_rule.h`, owner timing `pose_capture_rule.h`, engine calls `pose_game.cpp`, owner `pose_capture.cpp`, receiver `puppet.cpp` (hooks: `player_contact.cpp` hits on a puppet, `puppet_hooks.cpp` spine bend and rumble mute; think enter / exit `partner_think.cpp`; weapon re-equip `equip_refresh.cpp`); study and phase plan `docs/re/RE0_POSE_REPLICATION.md`, results `docs/RE0_NOTES.md` "Pose puppet" and "State mode: puppet body", one-PC test `adapters/re0/README.md`
- RE0 menu text and the Options screen (relabel a menu string, add a menu string, the Co-op entry in Options, why a chosen entry hung the menu): text `src/gui_text.cpp` + `src/gui_text_rule.h`, entry and router hook `src/options_coop.cpp` + `src/options_coop_rule.h`, page `src/coop_page.cpp` + `src/coop_page_rule.h`, drawn by `src/debug_overlay.cpp`; addresses `src/game.h` ("Menu text and the Options screen"); notes `docs/RE0_NOTES.md` "Menus: text hook and the Co-op entry in Options" (research: `coop-scratch/re0/MENUS.md`)
- Overlay contents: `adapters/re0/src/debug_lines.cpp` (add a stat: counter/gauge in `debug_stats.h`, update it where the event happens, add a line)
- SR2 co-op limits, addresses, the 8-peer replication switch (site tables in `replication_sites.h`) and the live trace lines: `docs/SR2_NOTES.md`, code in `adapters/sr2/src` (`coop_limits.cpp`, `limit_thunk.cpp`, `coop_pools.cpp`, `replication_switch.cpp`, `join_trace.cpp`); the player cap ("1/2", a refused third player): `player_cap.cpp`, notes "Player cap"; two players on one home network (silent join drop, wrong private IP): `home_network.cpp`, notes "Home network"; activities, diversions and strongholds with 3 or more players (sender scope, credit, targeting): `activity_sites.h`, `activity_*.cpp`, notes "Activities"; the join path trace (where a third join stops, `trace=1`): `join_net_trace.cpp`, notes "Join trace"
- SR2 partner logic for 3 or more players (what "the partner" means, a site's thunk, the owner-slot mapping, the live-test order): `docs/SR2_NOTES.md` "Partner logic", sites in `partner_sites.h`, thunks in `partner_thunks_*.cpp`, getters in `partner_scope.cpp`
- SR2 players 3 and 4 (milestone 9: sender scope, join admission, leave, pause, cutscenes, population, views, hand-over, traces; milestone 11: fast fire and the net clock in `net_clock.cpp` (notes "Fast fire"), cutscenes and leavers in `cutscene_sync.cpp` and `member_left.cpp`): `docs/SR2_NOTES.md` "Players 3 and 4", phases in `multiplayer_sites.h` (rows in `session_sites.h`, `world_sites.h`, `mission_sites.h`, `trace_sites.h`, `player_sites.h`), the code behind a row found by `multiplayer_thunks.cpp`
- SR2 a remote player not drawn, hidden after a cutscene, a missing skin (milestone 14): `docs/SR2_NOTES.md` "Remote player visuals (M14)", `player_visuals.cpp` (cutscene hide and restore, the skin pool), the `trace: remote human ... hidden .. render .. skin ..` line; the anim decode crash at 0xCE5199 (chunk anim slots, the streaming need table): notes "Script errors and fades", `anim_trace.cpp` (`trace: anim sets:` and `trace: anim:` lines)
- SR2 anim sets, stream requests and trigger character sets per remote with 3 or more players (milestone 16 W1: who a streaming_sync message goes to and runs for, the need table masks and size, a leaver's releases, the set change gates, the trigger presets): `docs/SR2_NOTES.md` "Anim and asset streaming per remote (milestone 16 W1)", code in `anim_stream.cpp` (sends, gate, masks, table move), `anim_stream_requests.cpp`, `anim_stream_triggers.cpp`, pure rules `anim_stream_rules.h`, sites `anim_stream_sites.h`, the `trace: anim sets:`, `trace: stream requests:`, `trace: set changes:` and `trace: trigger sets:` lines
- SR2 a remote player's character type or record, category 3, `styletest_pc_mp3` / `mp4` (M16 W1): `docs/SR2_NOTES.md` "One character type per remote", `char_types.cpp` (CT1 category 3 sizing, CT2 level load streaming, CT3 the char table fill), `char_types_rules.h` (`remoteTypeNumber`), the `char types:` and `trace: char types:` lines
- SR2 ambient traffic quotas, the vehicle census and the 64 vehicle pool (milestone 15): `docs/SR2_NOTES.md` "Traffic and the vehicle pool (milestone 15, B1 and B2)" and "Vehicle pool (milestone 15, B3)", code in `population.cpp` (W10b quota), `traffic_trace.cpp`, `vehicle_pool.cpp`, sites in `vehicle_pool_sites.h` and the generated `vehicle_pool_references.h`
- SR2 an outfit, an item or a weapon slot lock that reaches the wrong players, or a look not seen by player 3 or 4 (milestone 16 W1): `docs/SR2_NOTES.md` "Outfits, items and weapon slots (M16 W1)", sites in `outfit_sync_sites.h`, host code in `outfit_sync.cpp` (`TargetHold`, `SendToSyncTarget`), receivers in `outfit_sync_trace.cpp`, the Lua half `coop_sync_for` in `tools/sr2/lua/coop_outfit_sync.lua` and the 5 sites listed in `docs/SR2_M9_LUA.md`; the `trace: outfit sync:` lines
- SR2 3 or 4 players on one PC (echo testing, milestone 19): `docs/SR2_NOTES.md` "Echo testing (one PC, milestone 19)", the hub `tools/sr2/echo_hub.py` (rules in `echo_session.py`), the clone folders `echo_clones.py`, the checks `echo_check_table.py`; `python tools/sr2/echo_test.py`
- SR2 the mission start prompt, who owns a mission a guest asked for, the first accept that starts it, a retry or replay that picks the wrong remote, the replay prompt and its answers, the names in the waiting dialogs, the mission bonus counters of player 3 or 4 (milestone 16 W2): `docs/SR2_NOTES.md` "Mission flow (milestone 16 W2; MF1 to MF23)", sites in `mission_flow_sites.h`, thunks in `mission_flow_prompts.cpp` (start and replay) and `mission_flow_rewards.cpp` (bonus), rules in `mission_flow_rules.h`, AF6's tally key in `activity_framework.cpp` `declineEnds`.
- SR2 Crowd Control with 3 or 4 players (milestone 17 wave 3: the celebrity held by player 3 or 4, the start weapon, flags and warp per remote, the end release, flags and warp, the start and protect waits): `docs/SR2_NOTES.md` "Crowd Control (milestone 17 wave 3)", sites in `crowd_control_sites.h`, thunks in `crowd_control_select.cpp` and `crowd_control_each.cpp`, pure rules in `crowd_control_rules.h`
- SR2 activities and diversions with 3 or 4 players (milestone 16 wave 2: the participant pin of a race or escort, Snatch delivery and carry counts, Fraud zones and team hits, Mayhem and Trafficking weapons and credit, the streaking coat, the district objective, the AD3/AD4 trace): `docs/SR2_NOTES.md` "Diversions and activities (milestone 16 wave 2)", sites in `diversion_sites.h`, thunks in `diversion_*.cpp`, the pin in `activity_participants.cpp`, pure rules in `diversion_rules.h`
- SR2 FUZZ with 3 or 4 players (milestone 17 wave 3: the mode byte, the crime weapons, the crime area, the cameraman, crime NPC targets, the crime vehicle, the riot radius and the rioters): `docs/SR2_NOTES.md` "FUZZ (milestone 17 wave 3)", sites in `fuzz_sites.h`, thunks in `fuzz_thunks.cpp`, rules in `fuzz_rules.h`; the `trace: fuzz:` lines
- SR2 inline copies of the partner getter (walks of the player list) in the diversions and the 2+S activities, and the Mayhem destruction counters and score blocks with 3 or 4 players (milestone 17 wave 3: the walk rule, the Fraud start block once per remote, the Snatch cleanup and delivery stat, a bonus for each remote): `docs/SR2_NOTES.md` "Walks and the Mayhem counter (milestone 17 wave 3)", sites in `walk_sites.h`, thunks in `walk_thunks.cpp` and `mayhem_blocks.cpp`, pure rules in `walk_rules.h`
- SR2 the host's kick menu with 3 or 4 players (a picker of the remotes, then the game's confirm for the chosen one), the pause overlay's Disconnect (drops the remotes that hold the pause), the host's session end on a sign-in loss (removes every remote) (milestone 16 wave 1): `docs/SR2_NOTES.md` "Kick and session admin (milestone 16 wave 1)", sites in `session_kick_sites.h`, code and trace lines in `session_kick.cpp`, pure rules in `session_kick_rules.h`, the Disconnect rule `disconnectPausing` in `partner_thunks_session.cpp`
- SR2 minimap / pause map arrows, HUD prompts and per-remote GPS for 3 or more players: `docs/SR2_NOTES.md` "HUD and waypoints (milestone 16 wave 2)", `hud_sites.h`, `hud_remotes.cpp`, `waypoint_scope.cpp`, `tools/sr2/lua_patches/waypoints.py` (the per-script sends), `tools/sr2/lua/coop_waypoints.lua`, the `trace: hud:`, `trace: waypoint:` and `trace: lua: coop: waypoint` lines
- SR2 a remote that tags or does CPR drawn standing (the tagging pose 0x163, the Ambulance pose), ambient pedestrians popping in view of player 3 or 4, a follower bar missing for player 3 or 4, an all-players activity not warping the free remotes while the default partner is down, the crib garage door closing with a remote inside, the Hitman view cone of a remote, or `{CLIENT_NAME}` naming the wrong client (milestone 17 wave 3): `docs/SR2_NOTES.md` "Remote poses, world walks, garage door and client name (milestone 17 wave 3)", sites in `world_walks_sites.h`, code and trace lines in `world_walks_thunks.cpp`, pure rules in `world_walks_rules.h`
- SR2 downed players, revives, the respawn wait and notoriety with 3 or 4 players (milestone 16 W2: who can revive, when a downed player is killed, when notoriety is wiped, the revive pose anchor, guest notoriety locks, a crime or a vehicle of player 3): `docs/SR2_NOTES.md` "Downed, revive and notoriety (M16 W2)", sites in `revive_sites.h`, code and trace lines in `revive_thunks.cpp` and `notoriety_thunks.cpp` (D2 and D3 in `partner_thunks_gameplay.cpp`), reads in `revive_state.cpp`, pure rules in `revive_rules.h` and `notoriety_rules.h`, the `trace: respawn wait:`, `trace: downed local:`, `trace: team wipe:`, `trace: revive anchor:` and `trace: notoriety` lines
- SR2 homies, followers' second leaders, NPC sight, range and team tests, and the outnumbered-damage odds with 3 or more players (milestone 16 wave 2): `docs/SR2_NOTES.md` "Homies, followers and NPC AI (milestone 16 wave 2)", sites in `party_ai_sites.h`, thunks in `party_thunks.cpp`, `npc_ai_thunks.cpp`, `npc_team_odds.cpp`; the `trace: party:` and `trace: npc ai:` lines
- SR2 remote players drawn inside a store or garage, a car stored with P3 or P4 in it, or an activity level menu opening on the wrong machine (milestone 16 wave 2): `docs/SR2_NOTES.md` "Store, clothing and activity level menus (milestone 16 wave 2)", sites in `store_sites.h`, code and trace lines in `store_sync.cpp`, pure rules in `store_rules.h`; outfit and item sync is `outfit_sync_sites.h` (W1)
- SR2 Zombie Uprising with 3 or more players (start setup and placement, wave health average, arena zones, spawn view tests, waiting prompt; milestone 17 wave 3): `docs/SR2_NOTES.md` "Zombie Uprising for 3 to 4 players (milestone 17 wave 3)", sites in `zombie_uprising_sites.h`, code and trace lines in `zombie_uprising.cpp`, pure rules in `zombie_uprising_rules.h`

- SR2 the race resource hold, the mission replay start, the confessional (farthest and poorest remote, notices, lock), the session lost dialog name, `sync_from_player` (milestone 16 wave 2): `docs/SR2_NOTES.md` "Net handlers (M16 wave 2: RH, RP4, CF, SL, SN1)", sites in `net_handlers_sites.h`, code in `net_handlers.cpp` and `net_handlers_confess.cpp`, rules in `net_handlers_rules.h`, trace lines `trace: race hold:`, `trace: replay:`, `trace: confess:`, `trace: session lost:`; the Lua edit is in `tools/sr2/lua_patches/library.py`
- SR2 a remote that shares another's HUD bar, timer or objective, a mission callback that acts on every remote instead of the player who triggered it, a remote left without a vehicle seat or hanging a vehicle wait, name-keyed mission state (milestone 16 wave 2): `docs/SR2_M9_LUA.md` "M16 wave 2", the Lua half in `tools/sr2/lua/coop_slots.lua` (slots, other players) and `coop_seats.lua` (seats), the parameter rule in `tools/sr2/lua_params.py`, the patch data in `tools/sr2/lua_patches/`, the `trace: lua:` lines (slots, seats, locations) from `lua_trace.cpp` (`docs/SR2_NOTES.md` "Lua per-remote slots and LT1 (M16 wave 2)")
- SR2 silent Lua thread errors, black screens after cutscenes, fades (milestone 13): `docs/SR2_NOTES.md` "Cutscenes" and "Script errors and fades", the `trace: lua error`, `trace: fade` and `trace: cutscene` lines come from `script_trace.cpp` and `cutscene_sync.cpp`; the game's Lua has the base library only (`docs/SR2_M9_LUA.md`)
- SR2 Steam tunnel (the game's UDP through the launcher; `transport=steam|native`): `docs/SR2_NOTES.md` "Steam tunnel", adapter `tunnel.cpp` (thunks, inbox, member table), `tunnel_rules.h` (pure rules), `tunnel_sites.h` (exe edits), `game_socket.cpp`, `lan_address.cpp` (own address rule), launcher `games/sr2.json` (`transport` host option, `bridgeWhen`), `GameProfile.Bridged`, wire in `docs/CONTRACT.md` "Game messages of the SR2 adapter".
- SR2 force join (the launcher hands guests the host's session): launcher `NativeJoin.cs` and `sessionKind` `rendezvous` in `games/sr2.json`, adapter `force_join.cpp` (host and tick), `force_join_guest.cpp` and `guest_join_rules.h` (guest), files `coop\sr2_join.txt` / `coop\sr2_status.txt` (the join file's `name` line: `player_name.cpp`, `PlayerName.cs`, notes "Player names"), notes `docs/SR2_NOTES.md` "Force join"
- SR2 version gate (who may join, the trailer and content hash, the named refusal on the popup, the browser tag): `docs/SR2_NOTES.md` "Version gate", `version_gate.cpp` (gates, VG1 thunk), `trailer_rules.h` (pure layout, verdicts, texts, tag), `content_hash.cpp` / `content_hash_rules.h`, `browser_tag.cpp`
- Reverse-engineering patterns shared across games: `docs/re/PATTERNS.md` (grown by the `coop-re` skill)
- Enemy combat (guest hits on puppets, enemy hits on the partner, deaths): `enemy_combat.cpp` (net), `ds2/combat_hook.cpp` (engine), wire in `combat_wire.h`; notes `docs/DS2_NOTES.md` "Enemy combat"
- Entity activity / simulation focus (why enemies near the partner sleep, the second focus): `ds2/partner_focus.cpp`; spec `tools/ds2/out/analysis/STREAMING.md`, notes `docs/DS2_NOTES.md` "Simulation focus"
- Weapon sync (the body's weapon, shots, cosmetic damage drop): `weapon_wire.h` (wire), `weapon_sync.cpp` (net), `ds2/local_weapon.cpp`, `ds2/remote_weapon.cpp`, `ds2/damage_veto.cpp`; engine layout in `ds2/weapon_layout.h`; spec `tools/ds2/out/analysis/WEAPONS.md`, results `docs/DS2_NOTES.md` "Weapon sync"
- One-PC tests of RE0 state mode without a second player (what the game does when the other machine sends X, a test plan row as a command): `tools/re0/fake_peer/` (scenarios per `docs/TESTPLAN_RE0_STATE.md` row in `scenarios/`, message layouts in `wire.py`, gates and report in `gates.py`); start with `python tools\re0\fake_peer list`, check it with `python tools\re0\fake_peer selftest --smoke`
- Before pushing: `python tools/publish_check.py` (no game files, dumps, decompiled output or keys; also runs in the release workflow)
- RE0 enemies (design, who writes what, conditions: `docs/re/RE0_ENEMY_SYNC_REVIEW.md`): hits and deaths `enemy_net.cpp` (routing `enemy_damage_hook.cpp`), decisions `enemy_decision.cpp`, creation `enemy_spawn.cpp`, the follower's snapshot side (target, follow-start alignment, drift log) `enemy_state.cpp`, pure rules `enemy_follow_rule.h`, target `enemy_target.cpp`, door barrier `room_gate.cpp`, who owns a room's enemies `control_rule::runsEnemies` via `door_travel.cpp`
- Players in different rooms / independent play: `adapters/re0/src/split_rooms.cpp` (moving a character between rooms: `scene.cpp`), camera rule in `camera_parity.cpp`, control rule in `control_rule.h`; test on one PC with `tools/re0/fake_session.py`
- Tuning input delay without a partner: `adapters/re0/build/trace_replay.exe --synthetic 120 --jitter 60`, or record with `net_trace=1` and replay the file
- A crash on a player's machine: `coop\crash-*.dmp` next to adapter.log, read with `tools/re0/dump_stack.py`
- Launcher web page (WebView2): shell and browse screens `launcher/web/js/main.js`, motion recipes `css/motion.css` + `js/motion.js`, tokens `css/tokens.css`; screenshots without the launcher: `python tools/launcher_web/render.py <fixture|sequence> --out <dir>`
- Launcher web page, session screens (joining, hosting, lobby, mini, select), cards, steps, spinner: `launcher/web/js/cards.js`, `steps.js`, `session.js`; card state edge CSS `css/cards.css`; fixtures `dev/fixtures`, sequences `dev/sequences`
